Experiments show that our method matches per-scene optimization baselines in reconstruction and future prediction, while achieving stronger generalization to unseen interactions and objects with more consistent physical parameters.

</details>


### [36] [LMM-Track4D: Eliciting 4D Dynamic Reasoning in LMMs via Trajectory-Grounded Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19390)
*Chaoyue Li,Yongxue Xu,Jie Feng,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹引导的多轮时空对话新任务，构建Track4D-Bench基准（526个对话样本，23.5k帧，7.5k目标标注），并提出LMM-Track4D模型，通过RTGE（射线-时间几何编码）、专用流式状态令牌TRK和OSK-RA解码器实现4D连续时空动态推理，在基准上持续优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型在图像和视频理解上能力增强，但仍难以进行4D连续时空动态推理。为研究这一能力差距，作者定义了轨迹引导的多轮时空对话新任务并构建了相应基准。

Method: 提出LMM-Track4D模型，包含三个核心组件：1) RTGE（射线-时间几何编码）用于编码时空几何信息；2) 专用流式状态令牌TRK用于长时域动态传播；3) Object-Slot Kinematic, Residual-Anchor (OSK-RA)解码器用于在遮挡和视角变化下进行稳定的4步3D状态估计。

Result: 在Track4D-Bench基准上的实验表明，所提方法在强基线上持续取得改进，验证了显式动态状态建模对于激发LMMs中4D动态推理能力的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式动态状态建模是设计LMMs实现4D动态推理的有效设计原则，代码和数据集将公开。

Abstract: Recent large multimodal models (LMMs) have become increasingly capable on image and video understanding, yet still struggle to sustain 4D continuous spatiotemporal dynamic reasoning. To study this capability gap, we formulate trajectory-grounded multi-turn spatiotemporal dialogue, a new task in which a model must answer spatiotemporal queries while returning structured 3D target trajectories over an entire short clip or a specified segment of a longer clip, and introduce Track4D-Bench, a benchmark with 526 clip-level dialogue samples spanning 23.5k frames and 7.5k object annotations, for training and evaluation. Building on this task, we propose LMM-Track4D, which combines RTGE (Ray--Time Geometry Encoding), a dedicated streaming state token TRK for long-horizon dynamic propagation, and an Object-Slot Kinematic, Residual-Anchor (OSK-RA) decoder for stable 4-step 3D state estimation under occlusion and viewpoint variation. Experiments on Track4D-Bench show consistent improvements over strong baselines, suggesting that explicit dynamic state modeling is a useful design principle for eliciting 4D dynamic reasoning in LMMs. Our code and dataset will be publicly available at https://github.com/mikubaka88/LMM-Track4D.

</details>


### [37] [Neuron Incidence Redistribution for Fairness in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19393)
*Abin Shoby,Lyle John Palmer,Nikhil Cherian Kurian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现迁移学习模型中，末层主导激活通道会同时响应疾病阳性样本和特权群体（男性、老年），导致过度诊断；反之，劣势群体则被主导阴性通道激活，造成系统性漏诊。为此提出神经元发生率重分布（NIR）正则化方法，无需人口统计标签即可惩罚末层神经元激活方差，在HAM10000和OCT-RNFL数据集上显著降低TPR/FPR差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类模型在跨人口属性（年龄、性别、种族）时存在性能差异，但背后的潜在表征机制尚不明确。本文旨在识别这一机制并提出无需人口统计标签的公平性改进方法。

Method: 提出Neuron Incidence Redistribution (NIR)轻量正则化方法，通过惩罚末层神经元上经预测概率加权的平均激活值方差，迫使疾病证据分布在整个末层而非集中在少数主导通道上，且无需训练时提供人口统计标签。

Result: 在HAM10000上，年龄组TPR差距从10.81%降至0.93%，性别组从12.04%降至0.74%，AUC提升0.51点；在OCT-RNFL上，种族FPR差距从15.68%降至10.66%，年龄FPR差距从12.69%降至1.80%。

Conclusion: 将潜在疾病证据分布到完整末层是一种有原则且有效的医学AI人口公平性提升策略。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image classification are susceptible to subgroup performance disparities across demographic attributes such as age, gender, and race. We identify a latent representational mechanism underlying these disparities: in transfer-learned models, the dominant penultimate-layer activation channel under positive predictions is co-activated by both disease-positive samples and privileged demographic groups (male, older patients), producing over-diagnosis; conversely, the dominant channel under negative predictions is co-activated by disadvantaged groups (female, younger patients), producing systematic under-diagnosis. To address this, we propose Neuron Incidence Redistribution (NIR), a lightweight regularization method that penalizes the variance of predicted-probability-weighted mean activations across penultimate-layer neurons, requiring no demographic labels at training time. On HAM10000, TPR disparity drops from 10.81% to 0.93% across age groups and from 12.04% to 0.74% across gender, with a marginal AUC improvement of 0.51 points. On Harvard OCT-RNFL, NIR reduces FPR disparity for race (from 15.68% to 10.66%) and age (from 12.69% to 1.80%), demonstrating that distributing latent disease evidence across the full penultimate layer is a principled and effective strategy for improving demographic fairness in medical AI.

</details>


### [38] [Vision Harnessing Agent for Open Ad-hoc Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19410)
*Zilin Wang,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VASA，首个用于开放即兴分割的视觉驱动智能体。VASA无需训练，通过耦合VLM智能体、分割基础模型和视觉引导工作流，利用持久工作掩码进行推理、构建和验证分割结果。在PARS基准上超越SAM3 Agent 14-25%，在RefCOCOm上提升5-9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知概念的视觉分割已较容易（通过文本检索学习到的视觉定位），但开放即兴概念的分割仍然困难——这些概念可能不存在于单一学习掩码中，需要从图像证据中通过部件、关系、排除和集合等方式构建。

Method: VASA是一个无需训练的智能体系统，耦合了VLM智能体、分割基础模型和视觉引导工作流。核心创新是使用持久工作掩码（persistent working mask）而非仅修改文本提示，通过规划视觉操作、调用分割工具、检查结果、编辑掩码和错误恢复来逐步构建分割结果。

Result: 构建了PARS基准（将PartImageNet的部件级标签转化为长定义查询的开放即兴概念），VASA在PARS上超越所有基线（开放词汇、推理型、智能体方法），比SAM3 Agent高14-25%；在RefCOCOm多粒度指代分割基准上比SAM3 Agent高5-9%，比其他智能体基线高20%。

Conclusion: 验证了基于智能体的视觉构建方法在开放即兴分割任务中的有效性。指出为AI智能体编程任务知识、VLM行为、视觉例程、工作记忆和故障感知工作流是一条可行路径，超越了简单将基础模型包装为工具的做法。

Abstract: Segmentation has become easy when the concept is known, requiring retrieval of a learned visual grounding from text. It remains hard for open ad-hoc concepts, where the grounding may not exist as one learned mask and must often be constructed from image evidence through parts, relations, exclusions, and collections. We propose a Vision-guided Ad-hoc Segmentation Agent (VASA), the first vision harnessing agent for open ad-hoc segmentation. VASA is training-free and couples a VLM agent, a segmentation foundation model, and a visually grounded workflow. Rather than revising text prompts alone, VASA uses a persistent working mask to reason, construct, and validate a solution. It plans visual operations, invokes segmentation tools, inspects results, edits the mask, and recovers from errors. We construct PARS, a new benchmark that turns part-level labels in PartImageNet into open ad-hoc concepts through long-form definition queries. On PARS, VASA outperforms open-vocabulary, reasoning-based, and agentic baselines, surpassing SAM3 Agent by 14-25%. On RefCOCOm, a standard multi-granularity referring segmentation benchmark, VASA improves over SAM3 Agent by 5-9% and over other agentic baselines by up to 20%. These results validate agentic visual construction for open ad-hoc segmentation. Our work points to a path for AI agents beyond wrapping foundation models as tools: Programming them with task knowledge, VLM behavior, visual routines, working memory, and failure-aware workflows.

</details>


### [39] [Targeted Downstream-Agnostic Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19446)
*Zhuxin Lei,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对预训练编码器的目标性下游不可知攻击方法（TDAA），通过引入"威胁图像"作为特征级锚点，生成示例特定的对抗扰动，使编码器对对抗样本和威胁图像输出相同特征，从而实现更严格威胁模型下的定向攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下游不可知攻击（DAA）方法在宽松威胁模型下工作（仅需改变原始预测，无需特定目标），而更严格的目标定向攻击尚未被探索。由于下游任务未知且编码器不直接输出预测，实现定向攻击极具挑战。

Method: 提出TDAA方法，核心组件为"威胁图像"（攻击者预选的目标）。设计生成器为每个样本生成示例特定的对抗扰动，迫使受害编码器对对抗样本和威胁图像输出相同的特征表示，从而实现任务不可知的定向攻击。

Result: 在10种自监督方法、3个基准数据集上的大量实验证明了该方法有效性，揭示了预训练编码器的显著脆弱性。

Conclusion: 通过威胁图像作为特征级锚点，构建了任务不可知的桥梁来揭示编码器漏洞，示例特定的对抗范式相比传统单共享扰动方法具有更高的攻击成功率和隐蔽性。

Abstract: Recently, pre-trained encoders have gained widespread use due to their strong capability in representation extraction. However, they are vulnerable to downstream-agnostic attacks (DAAs). Existing DAA methods operate under a permissive threat model, where an attack is successful if the generated downstream-agnostic adversarial examples (DAEs) change the original prediction, without requiring a specific target. In this paper, we propose a Targeted DAA (TDAA) method under a stricter threat model requiring the attack to be both targeted and downstream-agnostic. Since the downstream task is unknown and encoders do not directly produce predictions, achieving a targeted attack is particularly challenging. To address this, we introduce a novel component termed the 'threat image', pre-selected by the attacker as the target. Specifically, a generator is designed to produce example-specific adversarial perturbations that compel the victim encoder to output identical features for both the DAEs and the threat image. Unlike previous DAA methods that generate a single shared perturbation for all samples, which often fails due to image diversity, our method adopts an example-specific paradigm. This generates tailored perturbations for each image to ensure a high attack success rate and invisibility. By leveraging the threat image as a feature-level anchor, our method builds a task-agnostic bridge to reveal the vulnerabilities of the victim encoder. Extensive experiments on 10 self-supervised methods across 3 benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach and reveal the pronounced vulnerability of pre-trained encoders. The code will be made publicly available after the review period.

</details>


### [40] [CutVerse: A Compositional GUI Agents Benchmark for Media Post-Production Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19484)
*Haobo Hu,Xiangwu Guo,Zhiheng Chen,Difei Gao,Haotian Liu,Libiao Jin,Qi Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Cutverse基准，系统评估GUI智能体在专业媒体后期制作（如Premiere Pro、Photoshop等7款应用）中的表现，涵盖186个复杂长周期任务。现有智能体仅达36.0%任务成功率，表明在长周期可靠性和领域特定规划方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体在网页导航和基础操作系统任务上取得进展，但在专业创意工作流（如媒体后期制作）中的能力尚未被系统探索，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建Cutverse基准，包含7款专业应用的186个复杂长周期任务；开发轻量解析器，将原始屏幕录制和低层级交互日志转换为结构化的组合式GUI动作轨迹。

Result: 现有智能体在真实媒体编辑任务上仅达36.0%成功率，当前模型在空间定位、多模态对齐和协调动作执行方面表现尚可，但在长周期可靠性和领域特定规划上存在局限。

Conclusion: 复杂长周期媒体后期制作工作流对现有GUI智能体构成重大挑战，该领域需要进一步研究以提升长周期可靠性和专业规划能力。

Abstract: While GUI agents have made significant progress in web navigation and basic operating system tasks, their capabilities in professional creative workflows remain largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce Cutverse, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate autonomous GUI agents in realistic media post-production environments. We curate expert demonstrations across 7 professional applications (e.g., Premiere Pro, Photoshop), covering 186 complex, long-horizon tasks grounded in authentic editing workflows, involving dense multimodal interfaces and tightly coupled interaction sequences. To support scalable evaluation, we develop a lightweight parser that transforms raw screen recordings and low-level interaction logs into structured, compositional GUI action trajectories with precise grounding. Extensive evaluations reveal that existing agents achieve only 36.0\% task success on realistic media editing tasks, underscoring the challenges posed by complex, long-horizon media post-production workflows in our benchmark.While current models demonstrate promising spatial grounding, multimodal alignment, and coordinated action execution, they remain limited in long-horizon reliability and domain-specific planning.

</details>


### [41] [EventPrune: Cascaded Event-Assisted Token Pruning for Efficient First-Person Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19506)
*Pengtao Ma,Ziliang Zhou,Ciyu Ruan,Haoyang Wang,Kaiyuan Li,Zihang Gong,Wenhua Ding,Chen Gao,Jingao Xu,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Event Cascade Pruning (ECP)，一种无需训练、利用事件相机高频运动线索引导视觉token选择的框架。ECP通过三级级联策略（事件触发因果采样、事件引导运动显著性过滤、事件注意力排序融合）在80% token缩减下超越全token基线（37.62% vs 36.31%），实现1.89倍推理加速和52% GFLOPs降低，并首次构建真实世界RGB-事件基准ESR-Real。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based Video-LLMs的二次注意力成本导致密集视觉token计算昂贵，现有token剪枝范式依赖离散静态快照，无法保留连续运动与几何线索，阻碍第一人称动态空间推理性能。

Method: 提出ECP框架，包含三个阶段：(1) Event-Triggered Causal Sampling (ETCS) 锚定运动信息丰富的关键帧；(2) Event-guided Motion Saliency Filtering (EMSF) 抑制事件不活跃的视觉token；(3) Event-Attention Ranking Fusion (EARF) 用运动显著动态校准空间注意力。

Result: 80% token缩减下ECP超越全token基线（37.62% vs 36.31%），推理速度提升1.89倍，GFLOPs降低52%。在ESR-Real基准上，ECP比全token基线准确率提升2.68个百分点。

Conclusion: ECP是首个无需训练、利用事件相机连续运动先验引导token选择的框架，有效解决Video-LLMs中密集token计算开销与动态信息保留之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: First-person dynamic spatial reasoning requires models to track continuous motion and precise geometric structure, but the quadratic attention cost of Transformer-based Video-LLMs makes dense visual tokens computationally expensive. Existing token pruning paradigms predominantly rely on discrete static snapshots, failing to preserve the motion and geometric cues essential for reasoning. We propose Event Cascade Pruning (ECP), to our knowledge the first training-free framework that leverages the high-frequency motion cues from event cameras as a continuous event-guided motion prior to guide token selection. ECP combines three stages: Event-Triggered Causal Sampling to anchor motion-informative keyframes, Event-guided Motion Saliency Filtering to suppress event-inactive visual tokens, and Event-Attention Ranking Fusion to calibrate spatial attention with motion-salient dynamics. With 80% visual token reduction, ECP outperforms the full-token baseline (37.62% vs. 36.31%) while achieving 1.89x inference speedup and 52% GFLOPs reduction. We further introduce ESR-Real, the first real-world RGB-event benchmark for first-person spatial reasoning, where ECP improves accuracy by 2.68 percentage points over full-token baselines.

</details>


### [42] [Return of Frustratingly Easy Unsupervised Video Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19510)
*Pengfei Wei,Yiqun Sun,Zhiqiang Xu,Yiping Ke,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MetaTrans，一种简单但有效的无监督视频域自适应方法，通过时空解耦和时序静态减法模块，在跨域动作识别任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督视频域自适应（UVDA）是一个实用但研究不足的问题，现有方法复杂度高且效果有限。

Method: MetaTrans采用仅含两个基本损失项的简洁学习目标，通过精巧的模型架构设计（时序静态减法模块）分别处理跨域视频的空间和时间差异。

Result: 在多种跨域动作识别任务上，MetaTrans取得了显著的绝对性能提升，且相对性能增益大幅领先现有UVDA基线方法。

Conclusion: 简洁的学习目标配合合理的架构设计可以有效解决无监督视频域自适应问题，时空解耦是关键思路。

Abstract: Unsupervised video domain adaptation (UVDA) is a practical but under-explored problem. In this paper, we propose a frustratingly easy UVDA method, called MetaTrans. Specifically, MetaTrans adopts a concise learning objective that contains only two fundamental loss terms. Despite the simplicity of the learning objective, MetaTrans embodies an advanced UVDA idea, that is, handling the spatial and temporal divergence of cross-domain videos separately, through a subtle model architecture design. By implementing a temporal-static subtraction module, MetaTrans effectively removes spatial and temporal divergence. Extensive empirical evaluations, particularly on various cross-domain action recognition tasks, show substantial absolute adaptation performance enhancement and significantly superior relative performance gain compared with state-of-the-art UVDA baselines.

</details>


### [43] [Are Watermarked Images Editable? SafeMark for Watermark-Preserving Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19511)
*Xiaodong Wu,Qi Li,Xiangman Li,Zelin Zhang,Lingshuang Liu,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeMark框架通过在扩散编辑器的训练目标中加入阈值化的水印解码损失，实现了文本引导图像编辑过程中水印完整性的保持，证明了语义可编辑性与水印完整性在本质上是兼容的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一个基础但未被充分研究的问题：带水印的图像能否在不损害水印完整性的前提下保持可编辑性。

Method: 在扩散编辑器的训练目标中显式加入阈值化的水印解码损失，微调编辑器使其在生成语义有效编辑的同时保持嵌入水印；该方法具有信息论解释——维持高比特精度下界了编辑器通道保留水印与编辑输出之间互信息。

Result: 在多个数据集、文本引导编辑方法和编辑后失真设置下，SafeMark实现了高水印比特精度，同时保持高质量语义编辑，且不牺牲对常见编辑后失真的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义可编辑性与水印完整性在本质上是兼容的，能够在生成式编辑流程中实现可信的图像溯源。

Abstract: This paper investigates a fundamental yet underexplored question: can watermarked images remain editable without compromising watermark integrity? We propose SafeMark, a framework for watermark-preserving text-guided image manipulation that explicitly integrates watermark integrity into the editing process. Specifically, SafeMark adds a thresholded watermark-decoding loss directly to the diffusion editor's training objective, fine-tuning the editor so that semantically valid edits also preserve the embedded watermark at the final output. This design admits a clean information-theoretic justification: maintaining high bit-accuracy on the edited image lower-bounds the mutual information that the editor channel preserves between watermark and edited output, the quantity that fundamentally controls watermark recoverability. SafeMark is compatible with differentiable diffusion-based editors, and requires no architectural modification. Extensive evaluations across multiple datasets, text-guided editing methods, and post-edit distortion settings demonstrate that SafeMark achieves high watermark bit accuracy across diverse editing settings while maintaining high-quality semantic edits, without sacrificing robustness to common post-edit distortions. These results demonstrate that semantic editability and watermark integrity are fundamentally compatible, enabling trustworthy image provenance in generative editing pipelines.

</details>


### [44] [iDiff: Interpretable Difference-aware Framework for Pairwise Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19522)
*Xinli Yue,JianHui Sun,Tao Shao,Liangchao Yao,Fan Xia,Yuetang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出iDiff框架，通过双分支设计（Answer Model用于偏好预测，Thinking Model用于理由生成）解决成对图像质量评估中的偏好预测与推理生成联合任务，在NTIRE 2026 RAIM挑战中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业摄影中的成对图像质量评估不仅需要识别出两张图像中更优的一张，还需要提供具有说服力的、基于图像的理由。现有的方法通常只关注偏好预测，缺乏可解释的推理能力。

Method: 提出iDiff框架，包含双分支设计：1) Answer Model：通过将每个样本分解为左/右全局和局部视图，结合内容感知专业化（针对人物和场景图像）以及基于集成的聚合机制进行鲁棒的偏好预测；2) Thinking Model：利用专家风格的模板、多源质量特征和基于Answer Model预测的答案感知监督，逐步增强理由生成能力。

Result: 在NTIRE 2026 RAIM挑战中获得第一名，在准确性和推理质量指标上均表现出色。

Conclusion: iDiff通过显式差异建模和结构化多模态推理的有效整合，为成对IQA任务提供了一种兼具鲁棒性和可解释性的解决方案，证明了分解式差分建模在图像质量评估中的有效性。

Abstract: Pairwise image quality assessment (IQA) in professional photography requires a model not only to identify the preferred image between two candidates, but also to provide convincing and image-grounded reasoning. In the NTIRE 2026 RAIM challenge, this requirement is further emphasized by jointly evaluating preference prediction and rationale generation. To address this task, we propose iDiff, an Interpretable Difference-aware framework for pairwise image quality assessment. Our method adopts a dual-branch design consisting of an Answer Model and a Thinking Model. The Answer Model performs robust preference prediction by explicitly decomposing each sample into left/right global and local views, followed by content-aware specialization for person and scene images and ensemble-based aggregation across backbones. The Thinking Model focuses on rationale generation and is progressively enhanced with expert-style templates, multi-source quality features, and answer-aware supervision conditioned on the Answer Model prediction. In this way, iDiff jointly models discriminative decision making and structured explanation, improving both robustness and interpretability. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework on both accuracy and reasoning-quality metrics. Our method achieved first place in the NTIRE 2026 RAIM challenge, showing the effectiveness of integrating explicit difference modeling with structured multimodal reasoning for pairwise IQA.

</details>


### [45] [Dual-Prompt CLIP with Hybrid Visual Encoders for Occluded Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19527)
*Zhangjian Ji,Shaotong Qiao,Kai Feng,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPL-ReID模型，通过双提示学习策略、真实世界遮挡增强和加权门控特征融合方法，有效应对遮挡人员重识别中的遮挡问题，在多个基准数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练视觉语言模型的人员重识别方法大多关注增强基于提示的特征学习，而忽略了遮挡物的语义信息，导致遮挡场景下匹配性能下降。

Method: 提出DPL-ReID模型，包含三项关键设计：1）双提示学习策略，利用文本提示捕捉完整行人语义并保持对遮挡的鲁棒性；2）真实世界遮挡增强方法，模拟真实遮挡场景以丰富训练样本；3）加权门控特征融合方法，结合LSNet捕获全局信息并作为特征门控机制，引导CLIP视觉编码器生成更全面的特征表示。

Result: 在多个基准遮挡ReID数据集上进行了大量实验，DPL-ReID达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: DPL-ReID通过利用遮挡物的语义信息和模拟真实遮挡场景，有效解决了遮挡人员重识别中的跨视角匹配难题，为遮挡ReID提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Occluded person re-identification focuses on matching partially visible pedestrians across multiple camera views. However, occlusions disrupt body-region cues, thereby complicating cross-view matching. Most person ReID methods built on pretrained vision-language models only focus on enhancing prompt-based feature learning while ignoring the semantic information of occluders. Based on the success of CLIP-ReID, we propose a novel Dual Prompt Learning ReID (DPL-ReID) model for occluded person ReID. It incorporates a Dual Prompt Learning (Dual-PL) strategy, which can utilize textual cues to capture complete pedestrian semantics and keep robustness against occlusion, and a Real-World Occlusion Augmentation (RWOA) method that realistically simulates occlusion scenarios encountered in real word to enrich occluded samples. In addition, we also design a Weighted Gated Feature Fusion (WGFF) method, which in corporates LSNet to capture global information and act as a feature-gating mechanism. This mechanism can effectively guide the CLIP visual encoder toward generating more comprehensive feature representations. Extensive experiments on several benchmark occluded ReID datasets show that our proposed DPL-ReID achieves the state-of-the art performance. The occlusion instance library are available at https://github.com/stone-qiao/DPL-ReID.

</details>


### [46] [Towards Camera-Robust 3D Localization: Equation-Anchored Tool-Use for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19528)
*Xueying Jiang,Wenhao Li,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang,Ran Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方程锚定的工具使用框架，通过在思维链中显式写出针孔反投影方程并代入工具输出的相机内参和深度采样值，实现多模态大语言模型在相机内参变化下的确定性3D定位，在3D目标检测和3D视觉定位任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在3D定位中受限于相机内参模糊性：同一图像在不同相机下对应不同3D场景。现有方法要么忽略相机参数、过拟合训练内参，要么借助外部工具获取深度和3D线索但仅作为参考提示，无法将相机信息确定性地传播到预测中。

Method: 提出方程锚定的工具使用框架：1) 主动检索相机内参并采样多点度量深度；2) 在思维链(CoT)中显式写出针孔反投影方程 $\hat{X} = (u_c - c_x)\bar{Z}/f_x$；3) 将工具输出代入公式，回归最终的9-DoF边界框。

Result: 在0.5倍到1.5倍缩放相机内参条件下，该方法在3D目标检测和3D视觉定位任务上均优于仅用RGB和工具增强的基线模型，在相机偏离训练尺度最大时提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过显式方程约束而非隐式学习，能够有效解决相机内参模糊性带来的3D定位问题，提升MLLM在变相机参数场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: 3D localization in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), including 3D object detection and 3D visual grounding, is fundamentally limited by camera intrinsic ambiguity: the same image admits different 3D scenes under different cameras. Existing MLLMs either ignore camera parameters and overfit to a canonical training intrinsic, or retrieve depth and 3D cues from external tools but treat the returned values as reference cues (numerical hints that the model is free to interpret implicitly), both preventing camera information from being deterministically propagated into the prediction. We propose an equation-anchored tool-use framework that re-purposes spatial tools as formula variables. The proposed framework proactively retrieves camera intrinsics and samples multi-point metric depths, writes the pinhole back-projection equation $\hat{X} = (u_c - c_x)\bar{Z}/f_x$ explicitly in Chain-of-Thought (CoT), and substitutes tool outputs into the formula before regressing the final 9-DoF bounding box. On both 3D object detection and 3D visual grounding tasks under rescaled camera intrinsics from $0.5\times$ to $1.5\times$, our method outperforms RGB-only and tool-augmented baselines, with significant gains where the camera deviates most from the training scale. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [47] [Boosting Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Core Token Attention-Based Seed Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19532)
*Yunzhe Zhang,Hongfu Liu,Pengyu Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ABSS方法，利用扩散模型早期去噪步骤中核心词元的交叉注意力动态来预测生成质量，无需训练即可在推理阶段筛选种子，显著提升文本-图像对齐和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型对随机种子高度敏感，不同初始种子会导致生成质量和图文对齐的显著差异，现有方法缺乏轻量高效的种子筛选手段。

Method: ABSS：在推理阶段，通过测量去噪前几步中核心词元（内容承载词）的交叉注意力动态来评估种子质量，对候选种子进行评分排序，保留top-k个种子用于完整生成，无需微调或修改初始噪声。

Result: 在三个基准测试上，ABSS在Stable Diffusion变体中实现了文本-图像对齐和视觉质量的一致提升，人类偏好和自动对齐指标均验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ABSS可作为现有种子优化流程的轻量级预筛选插件，在不改变噪声分布的前提下有效筛选高质量种子，且无需依赖固定接受/拒绝阈值。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can synthesize high-quality images, yet the outcome is notoriously sensitive to the random seed: different initial seeds often yield large variations in image quality and prompt-image alignment. We revisit this "seed effect" and show that attention dynamics over prompt core tokens, the content-bearing words, measured during the first few denoising steps, strongly predict final generation quality. Building on this observation, we introduce Attention-Based Seed Selection (ABSS), a training-free, plug-and-play method that ranks seeds for a given prompt by leveraging cross-attention to core tokens during the denoising process. ABSS requires no finetuning and does not alter the initial noise; it scores and ranks all candidate seeds, keeps only the top-k for full generation, and discards the rest, without relying on a fixed accept/reject threshold. Operating purely at inference time, ABSS can serve as a lightweight pre-selection add-on for existing seed-optimization pipelines, enabling additional gains. Across three benchmarks, extensive experiments show that ABSS enables consistent improvements in text-image alignment and visual quality for Stable Diffusion variants, as corroborated by human preference and alignment metrics.

</details>


### [48] [CaptchaMind: Training CAPTCHA Solvers via Reinforcement Learning with Explicit Reasoning Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19538)
*Pengcheng Wang,Haoxiang Liu,Yang Dai,Xiangxiang Zeng,Guanhua Chen,Baotian Hu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CaptchaBench基准（含16,000个带精细标注的验证码样本）和基于强化学习与推理过程监督的CaptchaMind求解器，在8类任务上达到82.9%的平均成功率，解决了现有方法在细粒度视觉细节捕获和区域比较上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAPTCHA求解方法缺乏大规模训练数据和过程级标注，导致在需要多步视觉推理和交互的现代验证码上表现不佳，亟需一个支持大规模训练的标准基准和相应的训练方法。

Method: 1) 构建CaptchaBench：程序化生成16,000个样本，覆盖8类任务，提供区域和过程级细粒度标注；2) 提出CaptchaMind：基于强化学习的求解器，使用显式推理过程监督进行训练。

Result: CaptchaMind在8类任务上平均成功率达82.9%，在真实世界实例上达71.0%，显著优于所有不使用闭源API的现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过大规模标注数据和推理过程监督的强化学习，可以有效提升CAPTCHA求解的视觉推理能力，为端到端网页自动化提供了关键支撑。

Abstract: CAPTCHAs are widely deployed as human verification mechanisms and frequently block intelligent agents from completing end-to-end automation in real-world web environments. Solving modern CAPTCHAs requires robust multi-step visual reasoning and interaction capabilities, yet training-based approaches have remained absent due to the lack of large-scale training data and process-level annotations. We introduce CaptchaBench, the first CAPTCHA benchmark designed to support large-scale training, comprising 16,000 programmatically generated samples across eight task categories with detailed region and process-level annotations. Systematic evaluation on CaptchaBench reveals that existing methods fail consistently on tasks requiring fine-grained visual detail capture and region-level comparison. We therefore present CaptchaMind, an RL-based solver trained with explicit reasoning process supervision, achieving 82.9% average success rate across eight tasks and 71.0% on real-world instances, substantially outperforming all existing methods without closed-source APIs.

</details>


### [49] [Trust It or Not: Evidential Uncertainty for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction with Trust3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19539)
*Zihao Zhu,Wenyuan Zhao,Nuo Chen,Chao Tian,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Trust3R 提出了一种轻量级证据不确定性框架，通过门控残差均值细化与 Normal-Inverse-Wishart 证据头，为前馈式 3D 重建提供具有概率解释的逐点几何不确定性估计，在多个基准上显著降低 AURC 和 AUSE，提升了不确定性排名与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前几何基础模型（如 MASt3R）的置信度评分为启发式设计，缺乏概率解释，无法可靠指示预测几何的置信程度及不确定性分布。

Method: 结合门控残差均值细化（gated residual mean refinement）与 Normal-Inverse-Wishart 证据头，输出封闭形式的多元 Student-t 分布，用于逐点几何不确定性估计。

Result: 在 ScanNet++ 上 AURC 降低 25%，AUSE 降低 41%；在多个室内外基准上持续提升风险覆盖（risk-coverage）与稀疏化（sparsification）指标，并改善几何精度。

Conclusion: Trust3R 提供了一种实用、概率化的可靠性信号，可有效用于下游几何管线的 uncertainty-aware 加权，且推理开销适中。

Abstract: Geometric foundation models hold promise for unconstrained dense geometry prediction from uncalibrated images. However, in current feed-forward designs, their predicted confidence scores are heuristic, lack probabilistic interpretation, and often fail to indicate where and how much the predicted geometry can be trusted. To address this gap, we present Trust3R, a lightweight evidential uncertainty framework for feed-forward 3D reconstruction. Trust3R combines gated residual mean refinement with a Normal-Inverse-Wishart evidential head, yielding a closed-form multivariate Student-t distribution for per-point geometric uncertainty. This design provides probabilistically grounded pointmap uncertainty estimates while adding moderate inference overhead. We evaluate on diverse indoor and outdoor benchmarks and compare against MASt3R's built-in confidence map as well as common uncertainty-aware baselines spanning single-pass heteroscedastic regression and sampling-based methods such as MC dropout and deep ensembles. Experimental results show that Trust3R consistently improves risk-coverage and sparsification, and generally improves geometric accuracy. These gains are reflected in stronger uncertainty ranking across benchmarks, with 25% lower AURC and 41% lower AUSE on ScanNet++, providing a practical reliability signal for uncertainty-aware weighting in downstream geometry pipelines. The project page and code are available at https://trust3r-z.github.io/.

</details>


### [50] [EgoCoT-Bench: Benchmarking Grounded and Verifiable Operation-Centric Chain of Thought Reasoning for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19559)
*Yang Dai,Dian Jiao,Tianwei Lin,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoCoT-Bench是一个用于评估多模态大模型在自我中心视频中细粒度操作推理能力的基准，包含3,172个可验证QA对，覆盖感知、回溯、预测和高层推理任务，实验发现现有模型存在答案正确但证据不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心视频基准缺乏基于证据的细粒度推理评估，很少检验模型推理过程是否基于明确的时空证据。

Method: 基于时空场景图(STSG)引导的生成框架构建3,172个可验证QA对，覆盖351个自我中心视频，分为4个任务组12个子任务组，并由人工标注者精炼。

Result: 多模态模型在细粒度自我中心推理上仍存在困难，许多模型生成的解释虽然答案正确，但证据与答案不一致。

Conclusion: EgoCoT-Bench可作为自我中心视频理解中基于证据的可验证推理的有用测试平台。

Abstract: The rapid development of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has led to growing interest in egocentric video understanding, specifically the ability for MLLMs to recognize fine-grained hand-object interactions, track object state changes over time, and reason about manipulative processes in dynamic environments from a first-person perspective. However, existing egocentric video benchmarks suffer from \textbf{limited grounded rationale evaluation}, offering limited support for fine-grained operation-centric reasoning and rarely examining whether model rationales are grounded in explicit spatio-temporal evidence. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{EgoCoT-Bench}, a fine-grained egocentric benchmark for grounded and verifiable operation-centric reasoning with explicit step-by-step rationale annotations. Overall, EgoCoT-Bench comprises 3,172 verifiable QA pairs over 351 egocentric videos separated into four task groups for a total of 12 sub-task groups, encompassing perception and retrospection, anticipation, and high-level reasoning. The benchmark is constructed through a spatio-temporal scene graphs (STSG) guided generation framework and is further refined by human annotators to ensure correctness, egocentric relevance and fine-grained quality. Experimental results show continuing difficulties with egocentric fine-grained reasoning and further reveal that many multimodal models produce explanations that are answer-correct, but have evidence that is inconsistent with the answer. We hope EgoCoT-Bench can serve as a useful testbed for grounded and verifiable reasoning in egocentric video understanding. Project page and supplementary materials are available at: https://dstardust.github.io/EgoCoT/.

</details>

### [51] [Decentralized autonomous organization and blockchain-based incentivization framework for community-based facilities management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18773)
*Reachsak Ly,Alireza Shojaei,Xinghua Gao,Philip Agee,Abiola Akanmu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链和去中心化自治组织（DAO）的智能建筑社区设施管理框架，通过去中心化治理平台和代币化激励机制促进利益相关者参与设施维护，经过成本、可扩展性、安全性、可用性测试及半结构化访谈评估，证明其作为激励解决方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统设施管理采用集中式决策结构，限制了利益相关者参与，导致与用户需求脱节、满意度降低。因此需要一种去中心化的、能够激励 occupants 积极参与的设施管理新方法。

Method: 提出了基于区块链和DAO的框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 去中心化治理平台——通过区块链投票实现透明的集体决策；(2) 维护管理平台——通过代币化奖励机制激励建筑 occupants 参与设施维护。系统评估涵盖成本分析、可扩展性、数据安全、可用性测试以及对设施管理者和研究人员的半结构化访谈。

Result: 评估结果表明该框架作为激励解决方案具有可行性，能够有效吸引利益相关者参与建筑基础设施的集体维护和改进。

Conclusion: 基于区块链和DAO的去中心化设施管理框架是一种可行的激励方案，能够促进利益相关者参与集体设施维护。但摘要未提及局限性或未来工作方向。

Abstract: Traditional facility management often relies on centralized decision-making structures that limit stakeholder participation, leading to misalignment with occupant needs and reduced satisfaction. This paper proposes a novel blockchain- and Decentralized Autonomous Organization (DAO)-based framework for community-based facilities management in smart buildings. The framework comprises two key components: a decentralized governance platform that facilitates transparent collective decision-making through blockchain-based voting, and a maintenance management platform with an incentivization mechanism that encourages building occupants to actively contribute to facility upkeep through tokenized rewards. System evaluation includes cost analysis, scalability, data security considerations, usability testing, and semi-structured interviews with facility managers and researchers to assess the platform's usefulness, challenges, and adoption potential. The findings demonstrate the framework's potential as a viable incentivization solution for engaging stakeholders in the collective upkeep and improvement of building infrastructure.

</details>
