Examples include sinusoidal inference functions for heavy-tailed distributions, interval-censored location inference, elliptically contoured models, and nuisance parameters via the Bhapkar--Godambe projection.

</details>


### [89] [Optimal Spectral Algorithms for Correlated Two-view Models in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19364)
*Hang Du,Henry Hu,Saba Lepsveridze*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对相关双视图模型中的高维推断问题，基于统计物理中的TAP启发式框架，统一处理了高维CCA、相关尖峰Wigner模型和相关尖峰Wishart模型，构建了无需先验参数信息的显式谱方法，实现了与信息论下界匹配的最优检测与弱恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维相关双视图模型中，现有方法缺乏统一的处理框架，且谱方法能否达到信息论最优阈值尚不明确。本文旨在通过TAP启发式方法建立统一理论，证明谱方法的最优性。

Method: 基于统计物理中的TAP（Thouless-Anderson-Palmer）启发式框架，构建显式谱算法，并在高维CCA、相关尖峰Wigner模型和相关尖峰Wishart模型三种经典设定下统一处理强检测与弱恢复问题。算法无需模型参数先验知识，仅依赖观测数据。

Result: 在三种模型下，所提出的谱方法均能达到强检测和弱恢复的对应阈值，且证明了匹配的信息论下界，表明谱方法在这些模型中的最优性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的TAP启发式框架具有广泛的统计适用性，证明了谱方法在高维相关双视图模型中的最优性，为该领域的理论分析提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We study high-dimensional inference in correlated two-view models, focusing on spectral methods for strong detection and weak recovery. We introduce a general framework, motivated by a TAP type heuristic from statistical physics, that provides a unified treatment of three canonical models: high-dimensional canonical correlation analysis, and the correlated spiked Wigner and Wishart models. Our main contribution is to construct explicit spectral algorithms in all three settings, that achieve strong detection and weak recovery down to the corresponding thresholds, where we prove matching information-theoretic lower bounds. Furthermore, our spectral procedures operate without knowledge of the model parameters, relying solely on the observed data. This demonstrates the optimality of spectral methods in these models and the broad statistical applicability of the framework.

</details>


### [90] [Influence as soft sparsity: Estimation of monotone functions on $\{0,1\}^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19648)
*Gérard Biau*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在布尔超立方体上从随机噪声观测中估计单调函数的问题。以总L¹影响度I(f)作为复杂度度量，证明了估计均方误差的极小极大上下界：下界为c·K²/(log n)^{3/2}，上界为C·K/√(log n)，其中K是影响度上界，n是样本量。上界通过自适应傅里叶阈值估计器实现，且均匀于环境维度d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在布尔超立方体上从噪声观测中估计单调函数的问题，并引入布尔分析中的总L¹影响度I(f)作为函数复杂度度量，该量对单调函数非负且控制估计问题的有效维度。

Method: 利用Friedgut's junta定理精神的光谱集中结果证明傅里叶谱集中在低度有影响力的坐标子集上；上界通过傅里叶阈值估计器实现并自适应于未知K；下界通过布尔超立方体中层的Varshamov–Gilbert packing结合Fano不等式建立。

Result: 建立了极小极大均方误差界：c·K²/(log n)^{3/2} ≤ inf sup E[||f̂ - f||²] ≤ C·K/√(log n)。上界对K≥1成立且在log d ≤ n^{1-ε}条件下均匀于维度d。

Conclusion: 给出了单调布尔函数估计问题的基本极限，上界和下界之间存在K·√(log n)因子的差距，表明该问题的极小极大最优收敛率尚未完全确定。

Abstract: We study the problem of estimating a monotone function $f:\{0,1\}^d\to[0,1]$ from noisy observations at uniformly random vertices of the Boolean hypercube. As a measure of complexity for the target~$f$, we use the total $L^1$-influence $I(f)=\sum\_{i=1}^d(\E[f(X)\mid X\_i=1]-\E[f(X)\mid X\_i=0])$, a classical quantity in Boolean analysis that is nonnegative for monotone functions and controls the effective dimensionality of the estimation problem: through a spectral concentration result in the spirit of Friedgut's junta theorem, the Fourier spectrum of any $f$ with $I(f)\leqslant K$ concentrates on low-degree subsets of the influential coordinates. We establish minimax bounds over the class $\cF\_K=\{f:\{0,1\}^d\to[0,1],\;f\text{ monotone},\; I(f)\leqslant K\}$: \[ c\,\frac{K^2}{(\log n)^{3/2}} \;\leqslant\; \inf\_{\hat f}\;\sup\_{f\in\cF\_K}\; \E\bigl[\|\hat f - f\|\_2^2\bigr] \;\leqslant\; C\,\frac{K}{\sqrt{\log n}}, \] where $n$ is the sample size. The upper bound holds for all $K\geqslant 1$ and is uniform in the ambient dimension~$d$ (under the mild condition $\log d\leqslant n^{1-\varepsilon}$). It is achieved by a Fourier thresholding estimator that adapts to the unknown~$K$. The lower bound relies on a Varshamov--Gilbert packing on the middle layer of the hypercube combined with Fano's inequality.

</details>


### [91] [Uniform projection designs under the stratified $L_2$-discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19900)
*Sixu Liu,Yaping Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了分层L₂-差异下空间填充设计的均匀投影准则ΦSD，推导了其显式公式、尖锐上下界及等式条件，证明了该准则可用于高效评估低维投影均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间填充设计准则多关注全维度均匀性，但低维投影均匀性同样重要。直接评估所有二维投影的分层L₂-差异计算成本高，需要一种更高效的统一投影准则。

Method: 针对U型(n,m,s^p)设计，推导ΦSD的显式公式（基于行对加权层次距离），建立尖锐上下界及等式条件，并通过数值研究验证准则的有效性。

Result: ΦSD可在O(n²m)时间内计算，相比逐投影评估显著减少运算量；许多已知最优构造能达到ΦSD下界；达到全分层L₂-差异下界的设计也达到ΦSD下界。

Conclusion: ΦSD是一种计算高效且能有效评估低维投影均匀性的新准则，为空间填充设计的选择和构造提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: This paper studies a uniform projection criterion for space-filling designs under the stratified $L_2$-discrepancy. The criterion, denoted by $Φ_{SD}$, is the average squared stratified $L_2$-discrepancy over all two-dimensional projections. For U-type $(n,m,s^p)$ designs, we derive an explicit formula for $Φ_{SD}$ in terms of row-pairwise weighted hierarchical distances, and we establish sharp lower and upper bounds with equality conditions. We further show that many known optimal constructions attain the lower bound of $Φ_{SD}$, and that designs attaining the lower bound of the full stratified $L_2$-discrepancy also attain the lower bound of $Φ_{SD}$. The criterion can be evaluated in $O(n^2m)$ time, with a modest reduction in arithmetic operations compared with direct projection-wise evaluation. Numerical studies illustrate the theoretical results and show that $Φ_{SD}$ is effective for assessing low-dimensional projection uniformity.

</details>


### [92] [Error Bounds for Importance Sampling with Estimated Proposal Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19989)
*Cathrine Aeckerle-Willems,Ilja Klebanov,Simon Weissmann*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文推导了基于数据驱动提议分布（如核密度估计）的重要性采样方法的非渐近误差界，将蒙特卡洛误差与提议分布近似误差分离，并为马尔可夫链生成的KDE建立了MIAE和MISE界，为实际应用中的防御性混合权重选择提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中广泛使用数据驱动提议分布进行重要性采样，但经典理论假设提议分布固定，无法解释层次化流程的统计性质，存在理论空白。

Method: 推导标准、防御性和自标准化重要性采样估计量的非渐近误差界；建立几何遍历马尔可夫链（平稳与非平稳）生成的KDE的MIAE和MISE界；将两者结合得到显式收敛速率。

Result: 误差分解为$n^{-1/2}$量级的蒙特卡洛误差和由MIAE/MISE度量的提议分布近似误差；获得了基于KDE提议的重要性采样的量化保证。

Conclusion: 为非参数重要性采样框架中防御性混合权重的选择提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Importance sampling with data-driven proposal distributions is widely used in practice. A common workflow first generates an auxiliary sample of size $N$ from an approximation of the target distribution, constructs a density estimate $\hat q$ such as a kernel density estimator (KDE), and then draws $n$ importance samples from this learned proposal. Despite its practical relevance, the theoretical properties of this hierarchical procedure remain poorly understood, since classical importance sampling theory assumes a fixed proposal.
  We address this gap by deriving non-asymptotic error bounds for standard, defensive, and self-normalized importance sampling estimators with random proposals. Our results separate the Monte Carlo error, scaling as $n^{-1/2}$, from the proposal approximation error measured through the mean integrated absolute and squared errors (MIAE and MISE) of $\hat q$. To obtain explicit convergence rates in $(N,n)$, we establish MIAE and MISE bounds for KDEs constructed from geometrically ergodic Markov chains in stationary and non-stationary regimes. Combining these results yields quantitative guarantees for importance sampling with KDE-based proposals. Our theory provides practical guidance for selecting defensive mixture weights in a nonparametric importance sampling framework.

</details>


### [93] [A Goodness-of-Fit Test for Independent Component Models in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20099)
*Mingshuo Liu,Siyao Wang,Miles E. Lopes*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在高维比例发散条件下具有理论有效性保证的独立成分模型拟合优度检验方法，该检验无需预白化步骤，并通过数值实验和基因表达数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立成分模型虽被广泛使用，但缺乏理论上有保证的拟合优度检验方法，尤其是在高维场景下，现有检验因依赖预白化步骤而受限。

Method: 开发了一种不依赖预白化步骤的拟合优度检验，在数据维度和样本量成比例发散的高维渐近框架下建立了理论有效性保证。

Result: 数值实验表明该检验在不同条件下能有效控制检验水平并具有统计功效；在基因表达数据上的应用展示了其实际诊断潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的检验方法填补了高维IC模型拟合优度检验的理论空白，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Independent component (IC) models are a standard tool for representing multivariate data in statistics, signal processing, and machine learning. Despite the extensive use of IC models, much less attention has been given to goodness-of-fit tests for assessing their compatibility with data. We develop the first goodness-of-fit test for IC models that is supported by a theoretical validity guarantee when the data dimension and sample size diverge proportionally. This is made possible by the fact that the test does not rely on a pre-whitening step, which often limits the applicability of other goodness-of-fit tests in high dimensions. Our theoretical analysis is complemented with numerical experiments that demonstrate the test's size control and power under a range of conditions. In addition, we provide examples involving gene-expression data to illustrate that the test has potential for effective diagnostic use in practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Accurate Evaluation of Quickest Changepoint Detectors via Non-parametric Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18798)
*Taiki Miyagawa,Akinori F. Ebihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非参数估计器KM-ARL和KM-ADD，通过将快速变化检测与生存分析进行类比，解决有限和不规则序列长度下平均运行长度和平均检测延迟的估计问题，并理论证明了其渐近无偏性，实验验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARL和ADD是快速变化检测中广泛使用的最优性准则，但实际数据集中的序列长度有限且不规则，限制了这些指标在真实场景中的应用。

Method: 将QCD问题与生存分析进行类比，基于Kaplan-Meier估计器思想提出非参数估计器KM-ARL和KM-ADD，用于在序列截断条件下建模检测概率。

Result: 推导了估计偏差边界，证明了在无需外推情况下估计的渐近无偏性；模拟和真实数据集实验验证了方法的实用性，增强了对有限和不规则序列长度的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法提升了模型的可解释性，便于经验性、直觉性的模型选择，并提供了开源Python实现供实践者使用。

Abstract: We propose non-parametric estimators for the average run length (ARL) and average detection delay (ADD) in quickest changepoint detection (QCD) under finite and irregular sequence lengths. Although ARL and ADD are widely used as optimality criteria in theoretical and simulation studies, their application to real-world datasets is hindered by limited and irregular sequence lengths. To address this issue, we propose non-parametric estimators for the ARL and ADD, termed KM-ARL and KM-ADD, by drawing an analogy between QCD and survival analysis to model detection probabilities under sequence truncation. We derive estimation bias bounds and prove that they are asymptotically unbiased unless extrapolation is required. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate their practical utility, enhancing robustness against limited and irregular sequence lengths, improving interpretability, and facilitating empirical, intuitive model selection. Our Python code is provided at https://github.com/TaikiMiyagawa/Kaplan-Meier-Average-Run-Length, offering ready-to-use implementations for practitioners.

</details>


### [95] [ScheduleFree+: Scaling Learning-Rate-Free & Schedule-Free Learning to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19095)
*Aaron Defazio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ScheduleFree+方法，一种无需学习率和训练调度的LLM训练方法，在长时间训练中性能优于WSD调度31%，为预训练中的模型平均和检查点合并提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schedule-Free Learning在标准基准测试中表现良好，但在大规模LLM训练中尚未验证有效性，需要解决扩展到大批量和大模型时的关键问题。

Method: 识别并修复了Schedule-Free Learning在大批量和大模型规模下的若干问题，提出了无需学习率和训练调度的ScheduleFree+方法。

Result: 在每参数1000 tokens的长时间训练中，ScheduleFree+相比SOTA的WSD调度性能提升31%。

Conclusion: Schedule-Free Learning最适合长时间训练，并为预训练中使用模型平均和检查点合并提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Schedule-Free Learning has shown promise as a practical anytime training method for machine learning, showing success across dozens of standard benchmark problems. However, strong performance for LLM training has only been demonstrated at small scales. We identify a number of fixes necessary to scale up Schedule-Free Learning to larger batch sizes and model sizes, and present a learning-rate-free and schedule-free method (ScheduleFree+) for training large language models which greatly outperforms Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD) schedules. We also demonstrate that Schedule-Free Learning is most effective for long duration training, and at 1000 tokens per parameter, it outperforms SOTA schedules by 31%. Schedule-Free Learning provides a theoretical foundation for the use of model averaging and checkpoint merging during pretraining.

</details>


### [96] [DeRegiME: Deep Regime Mixtures for Probabilistic Forecasting under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19231)
*Kieran Wood,Stefan Zohren,Stephen J. Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeRegiME提出了一种深度机制混合专家模型，通过稀疏变分高斯过程将时间序列中的潜在不确定性机制与信号分离，实现可解释的多步概率预测，在10个基准数据集上相比最强基线将NLPD提升了20.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经预测器存在两个关键局限：一是点预测丢弃了残差不确定性；二是概率预测头（如单一边际分布、未解释的混合分布、分位数集或扩散样本）很少揭示残差的机制结构。然而，含噪声异方差时间序列中的分布偏移可能表现为突变、渐变或依赖于预测步长，且往往体现在残差不确定性中而非条件均值中。

Method: DeRegiME框架包含：(1) 使用稀疏变分高斯过程（GP）作为核心，其非平稳机制混合核通过共享门控组合各子核与噪声过程；(2) Student-t似然函数处理重尾噪声；(3) 棒棒糖门控机制（stick-breaking gate）自动剪枝有效机制数量；(4) 直和特征空间表示，将机制锚定为残差相似性聚类，机制转换作为隐式变点呈现。该方法得到的是单个稀疏GP后验，而非混合GP专家。

Result: 在10个基准数据集和3种编码器架构上：负对数预测密度（NLPD）比最强编码器匹配基线（DeepAR/GluonTS风格的动态Student-t头）降低20.3%；连续排序概率评分（CRPS）改善3.0%；均方误差（MSE）改善4.7%。改进在所有数据集上一致，涵盖突变、渐变和季节性偏移场景。

Conclusion: DeRegiME通过显式建模残差不确定性中的机制结构，提供了一种可解释的均值-残差-噪声分解，在概率预测性能上显著优于现有方法，同时保持了计算可行性（稀疏GP）。

Abstract: We introduce DeRegiME -- Deep Regime Mixture of Experts -- a direct multi-horizon probabilistic forecaster that separates latent uncertainty regimes from the underlying signal and softly assigns each forecast location to learned recurring regimes using a sparse variational Gaussian process (GP) whose nonstationary regime-mixing kernel and Student-t likelihood combine per-regime sub-kernels and noise processes via a shared gate. This yields a single sparse-GP posterior, not a mixture of GP experts. DeRegiME addresses a key limitation of neural forecasters: point forecasts discard residual uncertainty, and probabilistic heads -- whether single marginals, uninterpreted mixtures, quantile sets, or diffusion samples -- rarely expose the regime structure of the residual. Yet distribution shift in noisy heteroskedastic time series may be abrupt, gradual, or horizon-dependent and often appears in residual uncertainty rather than the conditional mean. DeRegiME yields an interpretable mean-residual-noise decomposition with a direct-sum feature-space representation that anchors regimes as clusters of residual similarity whose transitions surface as implicit changepoints. The effective number of regimes is pruned by the stick-breaking gate. We prove kernel validity and predictive-density propriety, and across ten benchmarks and three encoder grids DeRegiME improves negative log predictive density (NLPD) by 20.3% over the strongest encoder-matched baseline, a DeepAR/GluonTS-style dynamic Student-t head, with parallel gains on CRPS (3.0%) and MSE (4.7%). Improvements are consistent across all datasets, which span abrupt, gradual, and seasonal shifts.

</details>


### [97] [EviTrack: Selection over Sampling for Delayed Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19283)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EviTrack 是一个测试时推理框架，通过在延迟消歧场景中维护多个轨迹假设并基于证据进行选择，优于传统的边际推断和采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在延迟消歧场景中，早期观测具有歧义性，多个潜在解释在证据积累充足之前都保持合理，而标准的边际推断方法要么过早坍缩不确定性，要么在信息证据到达后无法恢复。

Method: 提出 EviTrack 框架，在测试时对潜在轨迹而非边际状态进行维护，保持一组竞争的轨迹假设，并采用基于证据和似然比的选择机制，延迟承诺直到数据支持，灵感来自多假设跟踪和检测前跟踪。

Result: 在匹配推理预算下，EviTrack 显著优于基于采样的基线方法，在延迟消歧后实现更快的恢复速度。

Conclusion: 在延迟消歧场景中，适度的轨迹级选择比增加采样覆盖更有效，强调了"选择优于采样"作为可靠序列推理的关键原则。

Abstract: Sequential prediction is challenging in regimes of delayed disambiguation, where early observations are ambiguous and multiple latent explanations remain plausible until sufficient evidence accumulates. Standard approaches based on marginal inference struggle in this setting, either collapsing uncertainty prematurely or failing to recover once informative evidence arrives.
  We introduce EviTrack, a test-time inference framework that operates over latent trajectories rather than marginal states. EviTrack maintains a set of competing trajectory hypotheses and applies evidence- and likelihood-ratio-based selection to delay commitment until supported by data, drawing inspiration from hypothesis management in multiple hypothesis tracking and track-before-detect.
  To evaluate this setting, we construct a controlled synthetic benchmark with known latent ground truth that explicitly exhibits delayed disambiguation. At matched inference budget, EviTrack substantially outperforms sampling-based baselines, achieving faster post-disambiguation recovery.
  These results show that, in delayed disambiguation regimes, moderate trajectory-level selection is more effective than increasing sampling coverage, highlighting selection over sampling as a key principle for reliable sequential inference.

</details>


### [98] [Online Market Making and the Value of Observing the Order Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19584)
*Davide Maran,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线做市问题，在动作依赖的反馈模型下（未成交时揭示供需信息），提出了随机环境下的O(√T)遗憾界、均值回复价格过程下的O(√T)遗憾界以及对抗环境下的O(T^{2/3})遗憾界，验证了观察订单簿信息对提升在线做市学习效率的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习做市问题通常假设完全删失反馈（仅能观察到是否成交），而真实限价订单簿中未成交时能揭示供需信息。作者希望探究这种额外的动作依赖反馈如何改变问题的可学习性。

Method: 随机设定下提出基于淘汰的算法，无需交易者估值分布的平滑性假设；均值回复价格过程下分别考虑局部自回归动态和基于累积均值偏差的全局漂移条件，并依赖新的集中不等式；对抗设定下设计先探索后扰动的算法。

Result: 随机设定下达到高概率O(√T)遗憾；均值回复价格过程下同样达到高概率O(√T)遗憾；对抗设定下达到期望O(T^{2/3})遗憾。

Conclusion: 动作依赖的反馈（观察订单簿）能显著改善在线做市问题的遗憾界，与标准多臂赌博机反馈模型相比，即使有限的额外信息也能带来本质性提升。

Abstract: We study an online market-making problem in which a learner sequentially posts bid and ask prices for a single asset while interacting with traders holding private valuations. Unlike existing online learning formulations that assume fully censored feedback, we introduce an action-dependent feedback model inspired by real limit order books: when a trade occurs, the trader's valuation remains hidden, whereas when no trade occurs, informative feedback about supply and demand is revealed.
  We show that this additional information fundamentally changes the learnability of the problem. In the stochastic setting with i.i.d. market prices, we propose an elimination-based algorithm that achieves $O(\sqrt T)$ regret with high probability, without requiring any smoothness assumptions on the distribution of trader valuations. We then extend this result to a broad class of mean-reverting price processes by considering both local, autoregressive dynamics and a weaker global drift condition based on cumulative deviations from the mean. Under either assumption, we establish high-probability $O(\sqrt T)$ regret bounds, relying on a new concentration inequality of independent interest. Finally, in the adversarial setting with oblivious prices, we design an explore-then-perturb algorithm that guarantees $O(T^{2/3})$ regret in expectation.
  Our results quantify the value of observing the order book in online market making and demonstrate that even limited, action-dependent feedback can substantially improve regret guarantees compared to standard bandit feedback models.

</details>


### [99] [MiMuon: Mixed Muon Optimizer with Improved Generalization for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19619)
*Feihu Huang,Yuning Luo,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器的泛化误差，证明其泛化误差为O(1/(Nκ^T))，并提出混合动量SGD与正交化的MiMuon优化器，将泛化误差降低至O(1/N)，同时保持与Muon相同的收敛速率O(1/T^{1/4})，在Qwen3-0.6B和YOLO26m等大模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅分析了Muon优化器的收敛性质（优化误差），但其泛化性质（泛化误差）尚未建立，因此需要理论分析其泛化性能并提出改进方案。

Method: 基于算法稳定性（algorithmic stability）和数学归纳法推导Muon的泛化误差上界；提出混合Muon（MiMuon）优化器，通过谨慎使用梯度正交化，将Muon与动量SGD混合，牺牲部分收敛性能以提升泛化能力。

Result: Muon优化器的泛化误差为O(1/(Nκ^T))，其中κ是梯度估计的最小奇异值差，通常很小；MiMuon优化器的泛化误差降低至O(1/N)，且收敛速率与Muon相同，均为O(1/T^{1/4})。在Qwen3-0.6B和YOLO26m上的实验验证了MiMuon的高效性。

Conclusion: MiMuon在保持Muon收敛速度的同时显著降低了泛化误差，为矩阵结构参数优化器提供了理论与实践的改进方案。

Abstract: Matrix-structured parameters frequently appear in many artificial intelligence models such as large language models. More recently, an efficient Muon optimizer is designed for matrix parameters of large-scale models, and shows markedly faster convergence than the vector-wise algorithms. Although some works have begun to study convergence properties (i.e., optimization error) of the Muon optimizer, its generalization properties (i.e., generalization error) is still not established. Thus, in this paper, we study generalization error of the Muon optimizer based on algorithmic stability and mathematical induction, and prove that the Muon has a generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $T$ denotes iteration number, and $κ>0$ denotes minimum difference between singular values of gradient estimate. To enhance generalization of the Muon, we propose an effective mixed Muon (MiMuon) optimizer by cautiously using orthogonalization of gradient, which is a hybrid of Muon and momentum-based SGD optimizers. Then we prove that our MiMuon optimizer has a lower generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{N}\big)$ than $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$ of Muon optimizer, since $κ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we also studied the convergence properties of our MiMuon algorithm, and prove that our MiMuon algorithm has the same convergence rate of $O(\frac{1}{T^{1/4}})$ as the Muon algorithm. Some numerical experimental results on training large models including Qwen3-0.6B and YOLO26m demonstrate efficiency of the MiMuon optimizer.

</details>


### [100] [Latent Laplace Diffusion for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19805)
*Zinuo You,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLapDiff是一个生成式框架，将不规则多元时间序列建模为低维潜在轨迹，利用拉普拉斯域中的复共轭极点实现长时域预测，避免了离散重网格化和连续求解器漂移问题，在长时域预测和缺失值插补上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则多元时间序列在长时域预测中面临两难：离散方法通过重网格化会扭曲时间结构，而连续模型依赖顺序求解器易产生漂移。现有方法无法同时兼顾时间结构的保持和长程预测的稳定性。

Method: 提出Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff)生成框架：(1) 将目标建模为低维潜在轨迹，实现全时域生成而无需逐步积分；(2) 利用随机端口-哈密顿动力学指导逆过程，在拉普拉斯域通过可学习的复共轭极点参数化均值演化，支持在不规则时间戳上直接评估；(3) 通过更新平均分析建立连续动力学与不规则观测之间的联系，将采样间隔映射为有效事件域极点，并设计感知间隔的历史汇总器。

Result: 在长时域预测任务上优于多种基线方法，并且其连续时间生成性质支持在同一模型上通过查询历史时间戳进行缺失值插补。

Conclusion: LLapDiff有效弥合了离散与连续方法在不规则时间序列长时域预测上的鸿沟，同时兼具缺失值插补能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series impose a trade-off for long-horizon forecasting: discrete methods can distort temporal structure via re-gridding, while continuous-time models often require sequential solvers prone to drift. To bridge this gap, we present Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff), a generative framework that models the target as a low-dimensional latent trajectory, enabling horizon-wide generation without step-by-step integration over physical time. We guide the reverse process utilizing a stable modal parameterization motivated by stochastic port-Hamiltonian dynamics, and parameterize its mean evolution in the Laplace domain via learnable complex-conjugate poles, enabling direct evaluation over irregular timestamps. We also link continuous dynamics to irregular observations through renewal-averaging analysis, which maps sampling gaps to effective event-domain poles and motivates a gap-aware history summarizer. Extensive experiments show that LLapDiff improves over baselines in long-horizon forecasting, and its continuous-time generative nature supports missing-value imputation by querying the same model at historical timestamps. Code is available at https://github.com/pixelhero98/LLapDiffusion.

</details>


### [101] [FLUXtrapolation: A benchmark on extrapolating ecosystem fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19812)
*Anya Fries,Jacob A Nelson,Martin Jung,Markus Reichstein,Jonas Peters*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLUXtrapolation是一个用于评估生态系统通量外推能力的基准，专注于在逐渐加剧的分布偏移下进行预测。它定义了时间、空间和温度三种外推场景，并通过尾部误差和多尺度评估来挑战现有方法。初步实验显示，基线模型在常规指标上表现相似，但在提出的针对性评估下差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态系统通量是理解碳、水、能量循环的关键，但只能通过稀疏分布的观测塔直接测量。要生成全球通量估计，需要在观测站点上训练模型并使用全球可用的协变量预测未观测区域（即升尺度）。然而，升尺度面临严峻的域泛化问题：协变量分布在不同气候、生态系统类型和环境条件下发生偏移，同时还存在条件偏移（重要驱动因子在全球尺度上未被观测）。现有基准缺乏对这些偏移的系统评估。

Method: FLUXtrapolation基于通量升尺度的领域专业知识设计：定义时间外推、空间外推和基于温度的外推三种场景；性能评估跨越保留域、时间聚合和尾部误差三个维度。论文还对$P_X$和$P_{Y\mid X}$的偏移进行了定量分析。

Result: 在初步研究中，基线模型在每小时RMSE中位数上表现相似，但在提出的尾部聚焦和多尺度评估下显示出明显差异。

Conclusion: FLUXtrapolation为机器学习方法在分布偏移下提出了一个现实且相关的挑战，同时该基准的进展将直接支持改进通量升尺度的科学目标。

Abstract: We introduce FLUXtrapolation, a benchmark for extrapolating ecosystem fluxes under progressively harder distribution shifts. Ecosystem fluxes are central to understanding the carbon, water, and energy cycles, yet they can only be measured directly at sparsely located measurement towers. Producing global flux estimates therefore requires training models on observed sites using globally available covariates and predicting in unobserved regions, that is, upscaling. Flux upscaling is a challenging domain generalization problem that is affected by a shift in covariate distribution across climates, ecosystem types, and environmental conditions, as well as by conditional shift: important drivers remain unobserved at global scale. We provide a quantitative analysis of both these shifts in $P_X$ and $P_{Y\mid X}$. FLUXtrapolation is designed based on domain expertise on flux upscaling: it defines temporal, spatial, and temperature-based extrapolation scenarios and evaluates performance across held-out domains, temporal aggregations, and tail errors. In a pilot study, we find that baselines perform similarly under median hourly RMSE, but separate under the proposed tail-focused and multi-scale evaluation. FLUXtrapolation therefore poses a realistic and thus relevant challenge for machine learning methods under distribution shift; at the same time, progress on this benchmark would directly support the scientific goal of improving flux upscaling.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum Adversarial Machine Learning: From Classical Adaptations to Quantum-Native Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18821)
*Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Mohammad Meymani,Erfan Mahmoudinia,Dorsa Vazirzade,Peyman Paknezhad,Fateme Ghasemi,Saeed Saravani,Somayeh Nikkhoo,Kimia Haghjooei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇综述全面探讨了量子对抗机器学习领域，研究了量子机器学习模型在面对对抗性攻击时的脆弱性，以及现有的攻击方法和防御策略，同时回顾了理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和量子机器学习模型都容易受到对抗性攻击，随着量子计算和机器学习的最新进展，量子对抗机器学习领域应运而生，需要系统地研究量子机器学习的脆弱性、可能的攻击方式以及量子增强的防御策略。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统回顾和整理了量子对抗机器学习领域的现有攻击方法和防御措施，同时分析了理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战。

Result: 提供了量子对抗机器学习领域的详细概览，包括现有攻击和防御措施的全面梳理，以及对理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战的回顾。

Conclusion: 量子对抗机器学习是一个新兴且重要的研究领域，需要持续关注量子机器学习模型的对抗脆弱性，并开发量子增强的防御策略以构建鲁棒的量子机器学习系统。

Abstract: Machine learning has revolutionized numerous industrial domains. Despite recent advances, machine learning models remain vulnerable to adversarial threats. Adversarial machine learning is a field that studies these vulnerabilities to build robust machine learning models. Quantum machine learning is an interdisciplinary field that bridges quantum computing and classical machine learning. While quantum machine learning shows potentials to outperform classical machine learning in complex tasks such as regression, classification, and generative modeling, it remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Given the recent advancements in quantum computing and machine learning, the quantum adversarial machine learning field has emerged to study the vulnerabilities of quantum machine learning, possible attacks, and novel quantum-enhanced defense strategies. In this survey, we provide a detailed overview on quantum adversarial machine learning and explore the existing attacks and countermeasures. We also review the theoretical underpinnings of this area, emerging trends, and critical challenges.

</details>


### [103] [Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18829)
*Zibo Diao,Jingchu Gai,Xinyue Ai,Zhang Zhang,Zhenyu He,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling (LADS)，一种通过基于语义内容与用户查询次数确定私有种子的采样方案，在不影响良性用户体验的前提下，使得蒸馏者跨账户收集的数据产生相关性，从而从理论上和实验上证明能有效降低蒸馏学生模型的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿商业生成模型面临蒸馏攻击的严重威胁——攻击者通过收集模型生成响应来训练低成本竞品模型。现有防御手段要么修改输出损害良性用户质量，要么基于行为检测但易被多账户查询绕过。

Method: LADS通过语义内容与用户查询次数共同确定私有种子来生成随机性，使得同一语义桶内的不同账户共享潜在随机性，导致蒸馏者收集的数据产生相关性，从而降低样本多样性。

Result: 利用一致收敛理论证明LADS能降低蒸馏者泛化差距的收敛速率；在图像生成、数学推理和代码生成实验中，LADS显著降低了蒸馏学生模型的性能，同时保持了对个体用户的精确统计保真度。

Conclusion: LADS是一种无损、可证明有效的抗蒸馏采样方案，能在不牺牲良性用户体验的前提下有效防御多账户蒸馏攻击。

Abstract: Frontier commercial generative models face a growing threat from distillation, whereby a distiller harvests generated responses and trains a competing model of its own at drastically lower cost. Existing defenses either rely on modifying the models outputs, thereby sacrificing response quality for benign users, or on behavioral detection methods, which can be readily circumvented by distributing queries across multiple accounts. In this work, we propose Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling (LADS), a novel sampling scheme specifically designed to counter multi-account distillation while maintaining a lossless experience for benign users. Concretely, LADS derives the randomness underlying each generation from a private seed determined by the semantic content of the query and the number of times the user has queried the model. By construction, every benign user receives a response independently sampled from the original model at each visit, and thus experiences no distortion. In contrast, for a distiller, different accounts share latent randomness whenever their queries fall in the same semantic bucket. As a result, the harvested data becomes correlated, potentially reducing sample diversity and degrading generalization. Using uniform convergence theory, we show that LADS provably degrades the convergence rate of the distillers generalization gap relative to standard i.i.d. sampling in both unconditional and conditional generation settings. Experiments on image generation, mathematical reasoning, and code generation confirm that LADS substantially degrades the performance of distilled students while preserving exact statistical fidelity for individual users.

</details>


### [104] [Towards Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning on Sub-5KB Models: A Feasibility Study of Privacy-Preserving ECG Monitoring for Ultra-Resource-Constrained Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18862)
*Hangyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Family-FL，一种以家庭为隐私边界的层次化联邦学习框架，并设计仅669参数的Tiny CNN-LSTM模型（INT8量化后4.65KB Flash/2.95KB RAM），在MIT-BIH数据集上通信量仅为FedAvg的0.31%，准确率91.9%，实现了超资源受限微控制器上的隐私保护心律失常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球主要死因，可穿戴设备的持续ECG监测可早期发现心律失常，但联邦学习存在巨大通信开销，且标准深度学习模型无法适配超低功耗微控制器。需要一种既能保护隐私又能适配极端资源受限硬件的解决方案。

Method: 提出三层Family-FL架构：以家庭为自然隐私边界进行族内聚合，再进行全局同步。设计硬件约束下的Tiny CNN-LSTM模型（669参数），INT8量化后仅占4.65KB Flash和2.95KB RAM，适配STC32G12K128类微控制器。在MIT-BIH心律失常数据库上进行5次独立运行评估。

Result: Family-FL相比FedAvg通信量减少76.7%；Family-FL-Tiny总通信量仅为FedAvg的0.31%；准确率91.9±1.2%，宏F1为0.483±0.031；室性心律失常检测F1=0.80（临床最关键的异常类型）。

Conclusion: 通过仿真评估证明了在超资源受限微控制器上实现隐私保护联邦学习的技术可行性。但存在局限性：未进行硬件部署、仅单数据集验证（47名受试者）、罕见类灵敏度较低、缺乏正式差分隐私保证。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease remains the leading cause of death worldwide, and early detection of arrhythmias through continuous ECG monitoring on wearable devices can prevent life-threatening events. Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training by keeping raw ECG data on device, yet standard FL incurs prohibitive communication overhead and standard deep learning models cannot fit on ultra-low-power microcontrollers. We propose Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning (Family-FL), a three-tier architecture that uses the family as a natural privacy boundary for intra-family aggregation before global synchronization. We further design a hardware-constrained Tiny CNN-LSTM architecture with only 669 parameters, INT8-quantized to occupy merely 4.65KB Flash and 2.95KB RAM, meeting the constraints of STC32G12K128-class microcontrollers. Experiments on the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database (mean of 5 independent runs with different seeds) demonstrate that Family-FL reduces communication volume by 76.7% compared to FedAvg while maintaining comparable accuracy. Family-FL-Tiny achieves 91.9 +/- 1.2% accuracy with macro-F1 of 0.483 +/- 0.031, reducing total communication to 0.31% of FedAvg. The model achieves reliable ventricular arrhythmia detection (per-class F1 = 0.80), the most clinically critical abnormality for home-based preliminary screening. These results demonstrate the technical feasibility of privacy-preserving federated learning on ultra-resource-constrained microcontrollers through simulation-based evaluation. We honestly discuss limitations: no hardware deployment, single-dataset validation (MIT-BIH, 47 subjects), reduced rare-class sensitivity, and absence of formal differential privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [105] [Backdooring Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19262)
*Daniel Yiming Cao,Chengzhong Wang,Sheng-Yen Chou,Chengyu Huang,Pin-Yu Chen,Shengwei An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的训练时后门攻击，提出SHADOWMASK方法，通过修改前向加噪过程，将标准全掩码终端分布替换为触发-掩码混合先验，从而实现后门注入。实验表明该方法在多种模型和数据集上达到近100%攻击成功率，且保持干净性能、鲁棒性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）作为一种新兴的文本生成范式，其训练时安全性尚未被探索。现有的针对高斯扩散模型或自回归语言模型的后门攻击方法无法直接应用于MDLM，因为MDLM依赖离散状态破坏和迭代去噪，而非连续加噪或从左到右预测。

Method: 提出SHADOWMASK后门攻击方法。核心思路是修改MDLM的前向破坏过程：将标准的全掩码终端分布替换为触发-掩码混合先验。这创建了一条从触发破坏状态到攻击者指定目标的专用去噪路径，同时保留正常的干净去噪行为。论文还给出了形式化的数学推导，定义了后门前向过程、反向后验以及连续时间训练目标。

Result: 在基于DiT的MDLM和LLaDA-8B-Instruct上，对WikiText-103、OpenWebText和Alpaca数据集进行评测。SHADOWMASK达到近100%攻击成功率，显著优于标准数据投毒，干净性能基本保持，在全模型和参数高效微调下均有效，且对代表性防御方法具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SHADOWMASK是首个针对MDLM的训练时后门攻击方法，验证了MDLM面临严重的安全威胁。该方法攻击成功率高、保持干净性能、适应不同微调场景且能抵抗现有防御手段，揭示了MDLM安全研究的紧迫性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are emerging as a compelling new paradigm for text generation, but their training-time security remains largely unexplored. Existing backdoor attacks on Gaussian diffusion models or autoregressive language models do not directly apply to MDLMs because MDLMs rely on discrete state corruption and iterative denoising rather than continuous noising or left-to-right prediction. In this work, we present the first systematic study of training-time backdoor attacks on MDLMs. We propose SHADOWMASK, a backdoor attack that modifies the MDLM forward corruption process by replacing the standard all-mask terminal distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior. This creates a dedicated denoising pathway from trigger-corrupted states to attacker-specified targets while preserving clean denoising behavior. We further provide a principled mathematical formulation by defining the backdoored forward process, deriving the reverse-time posterior, and obtaining the continuous-time training objective. Evaluations on DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct across WikiText-103, OpenWebText, and Alpaca show that SHADOWMASK achieves near-100% attack success, substantially outperforms standard data poisoning, largely preserves clean utility, remains effective under full-model and parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and is robust against representative defenses.

</details>


### [106] [LLM Benchmark Datasets Should Be Contamination-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19999)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Jason Lucas,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基准数据集应当具备"抗污染"特性（即不可学习但可推理），利用Transformer架构训练与推理的不对称性，通过数学方法实现跨架构兼容，呼吁社区推进抗污染方法、平台和评估流程的建设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据集常被纳入预训练语料（即污染），导致无法可靠评估LLM的泛化能力，迫切需要一种不会被模型学习但可用于评测的数据集设计范式。

Method: 1) 指出基准数据集污染普遍存在并定义抗污染数据集特性；2) 利用Transformer架构中训练与推理流水线的不对称性实现抗污染；3) 提出数学进展使数据集在不同LLM架构间可互操作。

Result: 定义了抗污染数据集的理论框架，并提出三条行动路线：推进新方法论、开发支撑平台、将抗污染基准纳入现有评估体系。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区集体行动，通过抗污染基准确保LLM评估的可靠性和可复现性。

Abstract: Benchmark datasets are critical for reproducible, reliable, and discriminative evaluation of LLMs. However, recent studies reveal that many benchmark datasets are included in pretraining corpora, i.e., $\textit{contaminated}$, which diminishes their value as reliable measures of model generalization. In this paper, we argue that benchmark datasets should be $\textit{contamination-resistant}$, i.e., $\textit{unlearnable}$, but support $\textit{inference}$. To accomplish this, we first highlight the wide prevalence of benchmark dataset contamination and outline the properties of contamination-resistant datasets. Second, we highlight how the asymmetry between the inference and training pipelines in the Transformer architecture can be leveraged to support contamination-resistance. Third, we outline mathematical advancements to make these datasets interoperable across various LLM architectures. Based on the above, we call on the community to ensure the reliability of LLM benchmarking by: (i) advancing novel contamination-resistant methodologies, (ii) developing supporting methods and platforms, and (iii) adopting contamination-resistant benchmarks into existing evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [107] [SAGE: Scalable Automatic Gating Ensemble for Confident Negative Harvesting in Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20157)
*Sudheer Tubati,Amit Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE方法，通过SimHash分层采样和模块化门控集成技术，从无标签数据中识别可靠的负样本，以解决音乐流媒体欺诈检测中正样本-无标签学习面临的代表性偏差问题，在客户级和艺术家级欺诈检测中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐流媒体欺诈行为（通过虚假播放量操纵排行榜和版税分配）对平台和内容创作者构成严重威胁。传统欺诈检测方法难以区分真实用户（如超级粉丝、助眠音乐会话）与欺诈行为，因为二者活动模式高度相似。正样本-无标签（PU）学习中存在代表性偏差问题，即稀有行为群体的覆盖不足。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 基于SimHash的分层采样策略，通过保底约束确保稀有行为群体的充分覆盖；2) 模块化门控集成架构，采用可插拔的统计门（当前实例化为马氏距离和k-NN密度）配合可配置投票阈值，实现精确率-召回率的自适应权衡，从而从无标签数据中识别可靠的负样本。

Result: 在留出数据上实现了出色的精确率和召回率，且在无需修改核心方法的情况下，在客户级和艺术家级欺诈检测任务上均取得强性能，展现了跨欺诈检测领域的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAGE通过反事实感知的负样本挖掘方法有效解决了PU学习中的代表性偏差问题，在流媒体欺诈检测中表现优异，且具备跨领域泛化的潜力。

Abstract: Music streaming fraud, where bad actors artificially inflate stream counts to manipulate chart rankings and royalty payments, poses a significant threat to streaming services and legitimate content creators. Traditional fraud detection approaches struggle with a critical challenge: many legitimate edge cases, including super-fans and sleep-music sessions, exhibit activity patterns that closely mimic those of coordinated fraud. We present SAGE, a novel counterfactual-aware negative harvesting approach that combines SimHash-based stratified sampling with a modular gating ensemble for confident negative identification from unlabeled data. Our ensemble architecture employs pluggable statistical gates (currently instantiated with Mahalanobis distance and k-NN density) with configurable voting thresholds enabling adaptive precision-recall trade-offs. This addresses the representation bias problem in Positive-Unlabeled learning by ensuring comprehensive coverage of rare behavioral cohorts through floor-constrained sampling. Evaluation demonstrates strong precision and recall on held-out data. The approach generalizes across fraud detection domains, achieving strong performance on both customer-level and artist-level fraud without modification to the core methodology.

</details>


### [108] [Dimensional Balance Improves Large Scale Spatiotemporal Prediction Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18793)
*Jing Chen,Shixiang Pan,Yujie Fan,Haocheng Ye,Haitao Xu,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对时空模式分析中现有方法性能瓶颈且跨领域迁移性差的问题，本文通过空间和时间熵度量诊断时空复杂度不匹配，提出了一种可扩展的自适应框架ST-Balance。该框架通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度，并扩展时间视野以捕捉长程依赖关系。在城市交通、气象和流行病数据集上的实验表明，该方法取得了显著的精度提升和广泛的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空模式分析方法存在性能瓶颈，通常只能带来增量改进，且跨领域迁移能力有限。作者旨在诊断并解决这一瓶颈，实现更准确且通用的时空预测。

Method: 1) 使用空间和时空熵度量作为诊断指标，分析时空复杂度不匹配程度；2) 提出可扩展的自适应框架ST-Balance，通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度以保留结构信息，同时扩展时间视野以捕获长程依赖关系并缓解由时间异质性导致的累积误差。

Result: 在城市交通、气象和流行病三个不同领域的数据集上，该方法相比现有方法取得了显著的精度提升，并展现出广泛的跨领域适用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ST-Balance框架在多个时空预测任务上表现优异，具有良好的跨领域泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate spatiotemporal pattern analysis is critical in fields such as urban traffic, meteorology, and public health monitoring. However, existing methods face performance bottlenecks, typically yielding only incremental gains and often exhibiting limited cross-domain transferability. We analyze this bottleneck through spatial and temporal entropy measures, which are used as diagnostic indicators of spatiotemporal complexity mismatch rather than as guarantees that entropy alignment alone yields better forecasting. Empirically, larger mismatch is often accompanied by higher prediction uncertainty, especially under a fixed model-capacity budget. Guided by this diagnostic, we propose a scalable, adaptive framework that harmonizes spatial and temporal feature representations. Spatial dimensionality is compressed via low-rank matrix embedding to preserve essential structure, while an extended temporal horizon captures long-range dependencies and mitigates cumulative errors arising from temporal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on urban traffic, meteorological, and epidemic datasets demonstrate substantial accuracy gains and broad applicability across the evaluated domains, suggesting that the framework is promising for a wide range of spatiotemporal tasks beyond the current study. The code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/ST-Balance/ST-Balance.

</details>


### [109] [HELLoRA: Hot Experts Layer-Level Low-Rank Adaptation for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18795)
*Jia Wei,Zhonghao Zhang,Ping Chen,Qianyang li,Yancheng Pan,Shaoxun Wang,Ziyi Qiu,Longxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HELLoRA是一种针对MoE大语言模型的高效参数微调方法，通过仅将LoRA模块附加到每层最频繁激活的专家上，在减少可训练参数和计算量的同时提升下游任务性能。与LoRA在OlMoE上相比，使用仅15.7%的可训练参数、减少38.7%的适配器FLOPs、提升1.9倍训练吞吐量和9.2%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA及其变体主要针对密集架构设计，而MoE模型的稀疏激活模式为更高效的适配提供了未被充分利用的机会。作者希望利用MoE的稀疏激活特性，通过激活感知的适配器放置策略实现更高效的参数微调。

Method: HELLoRA仅将LoRA模块附加到每层最频繁激活的专家上；进一步与LoRI组合形成HELLoRI，冻结上投影并稀疏化下投影。这是一种激活感知的适配器放置机制，利用MoE的稀疏激活模式。

Result: 在三个MoE骨干模型（OlMoE-1B-7B、Mixtral-8x7B、DeepSeekMoE）和三个任务族（数学推理、代码生成、安全对齐）上，HELLoRA一致优于强PEFT基线。在OlMoE上相对LoRA：15.7%可训练参数、减少38.7%适配器FLOPs、1.9倍训练吞吐量、9.2%准确率提升。在DeepSeekMoE上仅用23.2%可训练参数即超越LoRA。

Conclusion: 激活感知的适配器放置是一种有效且实用的路径，可扩展MoE语言模型的参数高效微调。将适配器集中在高频激活专家上形成了一种结构化正则化，保护了预训练专家的专业化能力。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) dominates parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, yet most variants target dense architectures. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale parameters at near-constant per-token compute, and their sparse activation patterns create untapped opportunities for more efficient adaptation. We propose Hot-Experts Layer-level Low-Rank Adaptation (HELLoRA), which attaches LoRA modules only to the most frequently activated experts at each layer. This simple mechanism reduces trainable parameters and adapter-induced FLOPs while improving downstream performance, an effect we attribute to a form of structured regularization that preserves pretrained expert specialization. To stress-test HELLoRA under extreme parameter budgets, we further compose it with LoRI to form HELLoRI, which freezes the up-projection and sparsifies the down-projection. Across three MoE backbones, namely OlMoE-1B-7B, Mixtral-8x7B, and DeepSeekMoE, and three task families covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, and safety alignment, HELLoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines. Relative to vanilla LoRA on OlMoE, HELLoRA uses 15.7% of the trainable parameters, reduces adapter FLOPs by 38.7%, achieves 1.9x the training throughput, and improves accuracy by 9.2%. On DeepSeekMoE, HELLoRA outperforms LoRA while using only 23.2% of its trainable parameters. These results demonstrate that activation-aware adapter placement is an effective and practical route to scaling PEFT for MoE language models.

</details>


### [110] [UCCI: Calibrated Uncertainty for Cost-Optimal LLM Cascade Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18796)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UCCI提出了一种基于校准优先的路由器，通过等渗回归将标记级边际不确定性映射为每个查询的误差概率，并通过约束成本最小化选择升级阈值，在命名实体识别任务上以31%的成本降低实现了与大型模型相当的微F1分数（0.91），并将预期校准误差从0.12降至0.03。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM级联和模型路由方法大多使用未校准的置信度分数，且需要针对每个工作负载手动调整阈值，缺乏理论保证和自动化校准机制。

Method: UCCI采用校准优先策略：通过等渗回归将标记级边际不确定性映射为逐查询误差概率，并在三个显式假设下证明基于校准分数的阈值策略是成本最优的；等渗校准在预期校准误差上达到O(n^{-1/3})的样本复杂度；通过约束成本最小化自动选择升级阈值。

Result: 在75,000条查询的生产级命名实体识别任务上（使用4B和12B指令微调LLM，H100 GPU），UCCI在微F1=0.91时削减了31%的推理成本（95%置信区间：[27%, 35%]），并将ECE从0.12降至0.03。在相同操作点下，UCCI优于熵阈值法、分割共形路由和FrugalGPT风格的学习阈值方法。

Conclusion: 校准优先的路由策略能够在不牺牲性能的前提下显著降低LLM推理成本，且所有结果均基于实际模型输出和实测H100延迟，而非模拟路由或名义API价格。

Abstract: LLM cascades and model routing promise lower inference cost by sending easy queries to a small model and escalating hard ones to a large model, but most deployed routers use uncalibrated confidence scores and require per-workload threshold tuning. We present UCCI, a calibration-first router that maps token-level margin uncertainty to a per-query error probability via isotonic regression and selects the escalation threshold by constrained cost minimization. Under three explicit assumptions, threshold policies on the calibrated score are cost-optimal, and isotonic calibration achieves O(n^{-1/3}) sample complexity for expected calibration error (ECE). On a production named entity recognition workload of 75,000 queries served by 4B and 12B instruction-tuned LLMs on H100 GPUs, UCCI cuts inference cost by 31% (95% CI: [27%, 35%]) at micro-F1 = 0.91 while reducing ECE from 0.12 to 0.03. At the same operating point, UCCI beats entropy thresholding, split-conformal routing, and a FrugalGPT-style learned threshold. All cascade results use end-to-end routing on actual model outputs and measured H100 latency, not simulated routing from global accuracies or nominal API prices.

</details>


### [111] [Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18797)
*Rao Fu,Zixuan Yang,Jiankun Zhang,Jing Ma,Hechang Chen,Yu Li,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fully Looped Transformer，通过全循环架构和注意力注入两种免参数修改，解决了Looped Transformer在增加循环迭代次数时的训练不稳定问题（梯度振荡和残差爆炸），实现最多12次循环的稳定训练，并在下游任务上提升性能达13.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Transformer通过循环复用Transformer块，在不增加参数量的情况下利用更多计算提升性能，但存在循环迭代增加时训练不稳定的问题，根源在于梯度振荡和残差爆炸。

Method: 提出两种免参数修改：(1) Fully Looped Architecture：将跨循环信号分布到所有层以缓解残差爆炸；(2) Attention Injection：重用现有注意力块抑制梯度振荡。

Result: Fully Looped Transformer可稳定训练至12次循环，而基线模型在此条件下崩溃；在Looped Transformer不崩溃的较温和设置下，下游任务平均性能提升最高达13.2%。

Conclusion: Fully Looped Transformer提升了训练稳定性、下游任务性能，并通过推理时调整循环次数初步实现了测试时计算预算的自适应能力。

Abstract: Scaling model performance typically requires increasing model size. Looped Transformer offers a compelling alternative by iteratively reusing the same Transformer blocks, trading additional computation for improved performance without increasing parameter count or context length. Because the number of loop iterations can be adjusted at inference, it also provides a natural mechanism for balancing performance and test-time compute. However, Looped Transformer still suffers from training instability when the number of loop iterations increases. Our analysis reveals that this instability stems from two sources: gradient oscillation and residual explosion. To address these two problems, we propose the Fully Looped Transformer, which introduces two parameter-free modifications: (1) Fully Looped Architecture, which distributes inter-loop signals across all layers to mitigate residual explosion; (2) Attention Injection, which reuses the existing attention block to suppress gradient oscillation. These modifications stabilize training dynamics, enabling the Fully Looped Transformer to be trained stably up to 12 loop iterations, whereas other baseline looped models collapse in this regime. In milder settings where Looped Transformer does not collapse, Fully Looped Transformer still improves average downstream-task performance by up to 13.2\%. Overall, our experiments demonstrate that Fully Looped Transformer improves training stability, enhances downstream performance, and provides preliminary adaptability under different test-time compute budgets by varying loop iterations at inference.

</details>


### [112] [ReCrit: Transition-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Scientific Critic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18799)
*Wanghan Xu,Yuhao Zhou,Hengyuan Zhao,Shuo Li,Dianzhi Yu,Zhenfei Yin,Yaowen Hu,Fengli Xu,Wanli Ouyang,Wenlong Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCrit框架，将大模型与用户批评交互视为初始到批评的四种行为象限（纠正、谄媚、鲁棒性、边界），通过过渡感知强化学习提升科学推理中模型对批评的正确响应能力，在三个科学推理基准上将Critic准确率提升约13-14个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在科学推理中面对用户批评时，不仅可能回答错误，更会放弃原本正确的科学解决方案，将正确答案转变为错误答案。现有研究多聚焦最终答案准确性，忽视了交互过程中正确性的转变问题，亟需一种过渡感知的方法来区分有用纠正与有害谄媚。

Method: 提出ReCrit，一个过渡感知强化学习框架：(1) 将初始到批评的行为分解为四个象限：纠正（错误→正确）、谄媚（正确→错误）、鲁棒性（正确→正确）、边界（错误→错误）；(2) 奖励纠正和鲁棒性，惩罚谄媚，将持续错误作为弱边界信号；(3) 采用动态异步 rollout 和尾部自适应完成来减少 rollout 等待时间。

Result: 在ChemBench、TRQA和EarthSE三个科学推理基准上，ReCrit在Qwen3.5-4B上将平均Critic准确率从38.15提升至51.49，在Qwen3.5-9B上从45.40提升至55.59。消融实验表明，最终答案奖励提供的交互级增益很小，而过渡感知奖励和象限加权能产生更可区分的训练信号和更大的净Critic阶段改进。

Conclusion: ReCrit通过过渡感知的强化学习有效解决了大模型在科学推理中的有害谄媚问题，将错误交互转化为纠正和鲁棒性行为，显著提升了模型面对批评时的回答质量。

Abstract: Large language models can fail in critic interaction not only by answering incorrectly, but also by abandoning an initially correct scientific solution after user criticism. This is especially risky in scientific reasoning, where user criticism can turn a valid answer into an incorrect one. We frame critic interaction as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem, and identify three challenges: transition awareness, decoupling useful correction from harmful sycophancy, and scalable rollout. We propose ReCrit, a transition-aware reinforcement learning framework that decomposes Initial-to-Critic behavior into four quadrants: Correction, Sycophancy, Robustness, and Boundary. ReCrit rewards correction and robustness, penalizes sycophancy, and treats persistent errors as weak boundary signals. To make interaction training practical, ReCrit further uses dynamic asynchronous rollout with tail-adaptive completion to reduce rollout waiting. On three scientific reasoning benchmarks, ChemBench, TRQA, and EarthSE, ReCrit improves average Critic accuracy from 38.15 to 51.49 on Qwen3.5-4B and from 45.40 to 55.59 on Qwen3.5-9B. Ablations show that final-answer rewards provide little interaction-level gain, while transition-aware rewards and quadrant weighting produce more distinguishable training signals and larger net Critic-stage improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/black-yt/ReCrit .

</details>


### [113] [Theory-optimal Quantization Based on Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18800)
*Xiusheng Huang,Zhe Li,Xuanwu Yin,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BDQ（双向对角量化）后训练量化框架，通过优化矩阵变换分散离群值模式，在LLaMA-3-8B上实现W4A4量化精度下降<1%，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B的W2A4KV16任务上相比SOTA将性能差距降低39.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型量化中的激活离群值严重影响低比特精度性能，现有线性变换方法未能彻底解决离群值分布集中的问题。

Method: 首先建模量化误差与离群值间的数学关系，提出Flatness指标量化离群值分布，推导其理论最优解；进而提出BDQ框架，通过可学习的对角操作策略性地将离群值幅度分散到矩阵各维度上。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上W4A4量化精度下降<1%；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B的W2A4KV16实验中，相比SOTA将性能差距降低39.1%。

Conclusion: BDQ通过双向对角量化有效解决了离群值分布问题，在极低比特量化场景下建立了新的量化基准。

Abstract: Post-training quantization has emerged as a widely adopted technique for compressing and accelerating the inference of Large Language Models (LLMs). The primary challenges in LLMs quantization stem from activation outliers, which significantly degrade model performance especially at lower bit precision. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate outliers through linear transformations across feature dimensions, our analysis reveals that the transformed weights and activations still exhibit persistent outlier patterns with concentrated magnitude distributions. In this paper, we first model the mathematical relationship between quantization error and outliers, and then introduce a new metric Flatness to quantify the distribution of outliers. Based on this, we derive the theoretical optimal solution with respect to Flatness. Building on these insights, we propose Bidirectional Diagonal Quantization (BDQ), a novel post-training quantization framework that effectively disperses outlier patterns through optimized matrix transformations. BDQ strategically distributes outlier magnitudes across matrix dimensions via learned diagonal operations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDQ establishes a new quantization benchmark. It achieves less than 1\% accuracy drop in W4A4 quantization on the LLaMA-3-8B model. In the more challenging W2A4KV16 experiment, compared to state-of-the-art approaches, BDQ reduces the performance gap by 39.1\% on the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B model.

</details>


### [114] [PROWL: Prioritized Regret-Driven Optimization for World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18803)
*Ahmet H. Güzel,Jenny Seidenschwarz,Benjamin Graham,Jonathan Sadeghi,Jeffrey Hawke,Jack Parker-Holder,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种KL约束的对抗训练框架PROWL，通过策略主动暴露扩散世界模型的高误差轨迹并持续微调，结合优先对抗轨迹缓冲区，有效提升世界模型在罕见交互关键转换上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动演示数据系统性欠采样高影响交互区域（罕见但关键的转换），导致动作条件视频世界模型在这些关键转换上不可靠，而依赖自然发生难以覆盖失败模式。

Method: 1) KL约束的对抗策略训练，主动暴露世界模型的高误差轨迹同时保持接近行为分布；2) 持续微调世界模型于对抗发现的轨迹；3) 优先对抗轨迹缓冲区，基于预测误差、动作保真度和学习进展重新排序轨迹，聚焦未解决的失败模式。

Result: 在MineRL框架下的OOD轨迹评估中，PROWL提升了鲁棒性，揭示了弱行为约束下的奖励黑客行为，并验证了平衡探索性失败发现与显式行为正则化的必要性。

Conclusion: 可扩展世界模型不仅受益于更大数据集，更有赖于选择性生成信息丰富的训练数据。

Abstract: Modern action-conditioned video world models achieve strong short-horizon visual realism, yet remain unreliable on rare, interaction-critical transitions that dominate downstream planning and policy performance. Because passive demonstration data systematically under-samples these high-impact regimes, improving robustness requires actively eliciting model failures rather than relying on their natural occurrence. We introduce a KL-constrained adversarial curriculum in which a policy is trained to expose high-error trajectories of a diffusion-based world model while remaining close to the behavior distribution. The world model is continuously fine-tuned on these adversarially discovered trajectories, yielding an adversarial training loop that converts rare failures into a stable, near-distribution training signal without drifting into out-of-distribution exploitation. To maintain pressure on unresolved weaknesses as the model improves, we propose a Prioritized Adversarial Trajectory (PAT) buffer that re-ranks trajectories based on prediction error, action fidelity, and learning progress, focusing training on unresolved failure modes rather than repeatedly revisiting solved cases. We implement our approach in the MineRL framework and evaluate it on held-out out-of-distribution trajectories; PROWL improves robustness over models trained on passive data alone, reveals reward-hacking behaviors under weak behavioral constraints, and demonstrates that effective adversarial world-model training critically depends on balancing exploratory failure discovery with explicit behavioral regularization. Our results suggest that scalable world models benefit not only from larger datasets, but also from selectively generating informative training data.

</details>


### [115] [Block-Based Double Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18807)
*Asher Labovich,Benjamin Bradley,Vanessa Alexander,Chaitanya Harsha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于块的雙解碼器（block-based double decoders）架構，透過雙向因果塊級注意力遮罩，在訓練時實現完整損失監督與靜態序列打包，結合解碼器-only的訓練效率與編碼器-解碼器的推理效率。在規模定律實驗中，該方法顯著優於編碼器-解碼器模型，並緊追解碼器-only模型；推理時KV-cache記憶體與每token計算量至少減少2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 編碼器-解碼器模型雖在推理時比解碼器-only模型更節省計算，但其預訓練目標存在稀疏監督（sparse supervision）和動態序列長度問題，導致在大規模實踐中難以推廣。為了解決這兩難困境，需要一種既能保有解碼器-only訓練效率又能獲得編碼器-解碼器推理效率的新架構。

Method: 提出block-based double decoders架構，核心是採用doubly-causal block-based attention masks，使模型在訓練時能進行完整的損失監督與靜態序列打包，從而消除稀疏監督與動態序列長度問題。

Result: 在規模定律實驗中，block-based double decoders大幅優於編碼器-解碼器模型，且與解碼器-only模型的效能差距很小。推理時KV-cache記憶體和每token計算量至少降低2/3，且不影響prefill快取或其他現有推理優化技術。

Conclusion: block-based double decoders成功結合了解碼器-only的訓練效率與編碼器-解碼器的推理效率，為大規模語言模型的訓練與推理提供了一個兼顧效能與成本的新方案。

Abstract: Encoder-decoder models offer substantial inference-time savings over decoder-only models, but their pretraining objectives suffer from sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, keeping them out of practice at scale. We propose block-based double decoders, a novel transformer architecture that utilizes doubly-causal block-based attention masks to train with full loss supervision and static sequence packing, combining decoder-only training efficiency with encoder-decoder inference efficiency. In scaling law experiments, block-based double decoders strongly outperform encoder-decoders and closely track decoder-only models across scales. At inference time, they cut KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least 2/3 without sacrificing prefill caching or other existing inference optimizations available to decoder-only models.

</details>


### [116] [Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18808)
*Joao Paulo Cavalcante Presa,Savio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器分析了两个指令微调大语言模型（Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 2 9B-IT）中间层残差流中的文学原始特征，发现了命名门、第一人称自特征、文体风格调制器和组合情感四类特征，并采用5-LLM评委面板在27类情感分类上实现了高覆盖率，同时揭示了跨架构不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型内部是否具备可解释的文学/情感原始特征，以及这些特征如何组合产生情感表达，理解模型在文学性文本生成中的内部表征机制。

Method: 在中间深度残差流上应用稀疏自编码器提取特征；采用三阶段验证流程（logit-lens、LLM-rate、5-LLM评委面板）；使用27类情感分类（Cowen-Keltner）进行强制选择判断；通过单特征和多特征组合引导进行实验。

Result: Llama达到27/27全覆盖，Gemma达到23/27（唯一严格失败为adoration）；随机判断下单细胞通过概率约10^{-3}，双种子假阳性期望可忽略；跨架构不对称性表现为Llama直接命名目标情感而Gemma通过场景意象间接唤起。

Conclusion: 两个架构均包含同时作为语域标记和情感发射器的自特征（包括RLHF加载最重的单一自特征）；展示了单GPU、每情感特征发现约15分钟的高效可解释性分析流程。

Abstract: We characterize a compositional architecture of literary primitives in two instruction-tuned large language models (Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT) via sparse autoencoders on mid-depth residual streams. Four feature classes emerge: naming-gates that promote lexical tokens of a target affect, an eleven-self cluster of first-person register features, stylistic register modulators (show-don't-tell and defamiliarization), and compositional emotions that arise only from multi-feature steering. Under a forced-choice 5-LLM judge panel applied to a 27-category emotion taxonomy (Cowen-Keltner), Llama reaches full 27/27 coverage by combining naming-gates, multi-feature recipes, and single self-feature steering; Gemma reaches 23/27 with adoration as the single residual strict-fail. Under random judging, the per-cell pass probability is on the order of $10^{-3}$ and the expected number of two-seed false-positive cells across the catalog is negligible, so the observed coverage is not consistent with chance. A cross-architectural asymmetry sits in the strict-versus-soft judge contrast: on the same generations, judges agree more often on Llama outputs than on Gemma outputs because Llama outputs name the target affect more directly while Gemma outputs evoke it through scene and imagery. Both architectures contain self-features that serve simultaneously as register markers and as emotion emitters, including a single most-RLHF-loaded self-feature per architecture that intensifies the institutional Helper-AI persona at one operating regime and produces affect-categorizable output at the same calibrated coefficient. Methodologically, the paper presents a three-stage validation pipeline (logit-lens, LLM-rate, 5-LLM judge) with documented anti-patterns; the total compute is single-GPU and about 15 minutes per emotion-feature discovery cycle.

</details>


### [117] [Metric-Gradient Projection for Stable Multi-Agent Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18809)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Sizhe Tang,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HPML通过Hodge投影将多智能体系统的联合更新场分解为度量梯度势流和非势部分，并沿投影后的度量梯度方向更新，从而解耦智能体间的循环交互动力学，提升MARL的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体学习中各智能体的策略更新相互耦合，导致联合更新场中存在不可积分的循环交互分量，使得学习缓慢或不稳定。现有方法（正则化、信用分配、共识方法）通过局部或算法修改来稳定MARL，但缺乏从几何角度解耦联合更新场的全局视角。

Method: 将多智能体系统的联合更新场视为L²空间中的向量场，通过Hodge投影将其分解为度量梯度势流（可积部分）和循环/散度部分（非势部分），仅沿投影后的度量梯度分量更新。投影通过变分定义、泊松型方程刻画，并由基于图的和摊销的神经实现从样本中恢复投影方向。

Result: 投影后的动力学具有Lyapunov势函数，并给出了带有显式非势性项的均衡间隙界。控制实验验证了几何机制的有效性，CTDE基准测试表明HPML作为即插即用投影层可提升稳定性和归一化回报。

Conclusion: HPML从几何角度解耦了多智能体学习中的循环交互动力学，提供了一种即插即用的投影层方法，有效提升了MARL的稳定性与性能，并给出了理论保证。

Abstract: General-sum multi-agent learning is often governed by a stacked update field in which each agent's policy update changes the optimization landscape faced by the others. This coupling can entangle an integrable component of collective improvement with cyclic interaction dynamics, leading to slow or unstable multi-agent learning. Existing approaches, such as regularization, credit assignment, and consensus methods, stabilize MARL through local or algorithmic modifications; HPML complements them by projecting the joint update field onto a metric-gradient component. We introduce \textbf{HPML} (\textbf{H}odge-\textbf{P}rojected \textbf{M}ulti-agent \textbf{L}earning), which views the joint update field of a multi-agent system as an element of an $L^2$ space of vector fields and computes a Hodge-type projection onto the closest metric-gradient potential flow. HPML follows the projected component as the update direction, yielding the closest metric-gradient field under the chosen metric and sampling measure. The projection is defined variationally, characterized by a Poisson-type equation, and implemented through graph-based and amortized neural realizations that recover projected directions from samples. We show that the projected dynamics admit a Lyapunov potential and yield equilibrium-gap bounds with an explicit additive non-potentiality term. Controlled experiments validate the geometric mechanism, and CTDE benchmarks show improved stability and normalized return when HPML is used as a plug-in projection layer in MARL pipelines.

</details>


### [118] [PASC: Pipeline-Aware Conformal Prediction with Joint Coverage Guarantees for Multi-Stage NLP and LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18812)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PASC提出了一种面向多阶段管道的联合共形预测方法，将多阶段的联合覆盖保证简化为单个标量共形预测问题，在有限样本下无分布假设地保证所有K个阶段同时被覆盖的概率至少为1-α，并在NER→NED→实体类型标注等管道上优于Bonferroni校正和独立共形预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP和LLM系统采用多阶段管道架构（如NER→NED→实体类型标注、检索增强生成、智能体链），各阶段误差会累积传播。但现有不确定性量化方法要么独立校准每个阶段（无法提供联合覆盖保证），要么采用Bonferroni联合界（提供联合覆盖但过于保守），缺乏既能提供精确联合覆盖保证又不过于保守的方法。

Method: 提出PASC（管道感知分离共形预测），将多阶段联合覆盖问题简化为联合最大非一致性分数上的单个标量共形预测问题。通过在所有阶段上取最大非一致性分数作为联合统计量，只需一次分位数计算即可获得所有K个阶段的同时覆盖保证，且紧度仅差1/(n+1)因子。

Result: 在三阶段NER→NED→实体类型标注管道上（CoNLL-2003），PASC达到96.4%端到端覆盖率，优于Bonferroni的93.4%和独立CP的86.5%，且平均预测集大小相同（1.083）。在分布漂移场景下（WNUT-17 Twitter和WikiNEuRal Wikipedia），PASC经验性地维持目标覆盖率，而独立CP降至59%。PASC只需一次分位数计算，速度比Bonferroni快1.7倍，可扩展至K=6阶段（独立CP端到端覆盖率降至0.53）。

Conclusion: PASC提供了一种简洁高效的多阶段管道联合覆盖保证方法，可直接应用于复合LLM系统和智能体管道，在保持较高覆盖率和计算效率的同时，对分布漂移具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern NLP and LLM systems are pipelines: named entity recognition (NER) -> entity disambiguation (NED) -> entity typing, retrieval-augmented generation (retriever -> reader), and agentic chains of planner -> tool -> critic. Errors compound across stages, but existing uncertainty quantification methods either calibrate each stage independently (no joint coverage) or apply a Bonferroni union bound (joint coverage, but conservative). We present PASC (Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal), which reduces multi-stage joint coverage to a single scalar conformal prediction problem on the joint maximum nonconformity score. PASC provides a finite-sample distribution-free guarantee that all K stages are simultaneously covered with probability at least 1 - alpha, and is nearly tight up to a 1/(n+1) factor. On a three-stage NER -> NED -> entity-typing pipeline over CoNLL-2003, PASC achieves 96.4% end-to-end coverage versus 93.4% for Bonferroni and 86.5% for independent CP, at identical average prediction set size (1.083). Under distribution shift to WNUT-17 Twitter and WikiNEuRal Wikipedia data, PASC empirically maintains the target coverage in the tested shift settings while independent CP collapses to 59%. PASC requires a single quantile computation, runs 1.7x faster than Bonferroni, and scales to K = 6 stages where independent CP drops to 0.53 end-to-end coverage. The same joint-maximum-score reduction applies directly to compound LLM systems and agent pipelines.

</details>


### [119] [General Lower Bounds for Differentially Private Federated Learning with Arbitrary Public-Transcript Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19813)
*Yicheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私联邦学习协议，提出了一种基于完全公开交互转录本的通用下界，并建立了联邦van Trees下界，适用于满足零集中差分隐私（zCDP）约束的估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习隐私分析缺乏针对任意多轮交互协议的一般性下界，特别是当客户端本地样本可在多轮中重复使用时。作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过提出隐私-信息收缩不等式（privacy-information contraction inequality）用于完全公开转录本，结合van Trees不等式，推导出联邦场景下满足zCDP约束的估计器的通用下界。

Result: 得到了一个联邦van Trees下界，该下界适用于任何满足总客户端逐样本级zCDP约束的估计器，并在均值估计、线性回归和非参数回归中展示了具体应用。

Conclusion: 该下界为理解差分隐私联邦学习中通信轮次、隐私预算与估计精度之间的基本权衡提供了理论基础，且适用于任意自适应多轮交互协议。

Abstract: We prove a general lower bound for differentially private federated learning protocols with arbitrary public-transcript interactions. The protocol may use any number of adaptive rounds, and each client's local samples may be reused across rounds. For parameter estimation under squared \(\ell_2\) loss, we establish a federated van Trees lower bound for every estimator satisfying a total clientwise sample-level zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) constraint. The main technical ingredient is a privacy-information contraction inequality for complete public transcripts. We illustrate the bound through applications to mean estimation, linear regression, and nonparametric regression.

</details>


### [120] [Composition of Memory Experts for Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18813)
*Sebastian Stapf,Pablo Acuaviva Huertos,Aram Davtyan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散框架的专家混合世界模型，通过对比产品-of-experts公式整合三种互补记忆专家（短期局部动态、长期外部权重微调、空间几何一致性），在不产生二次成本的情况下提升长程时间一致性和导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型架构面临记忆权衡：Transformer保留局部细节但受二次注意力瓶颈限制，循环和状态空间模型扩展性好但压缩历史导致保真度损失。作者希望打破单一架构限制，解耦未来-过去一致性。

Method: 采用扩散框架，通过对比产品-of-experts公式集成三种异构记忆模型：1) 短期记忆专家捕捉细粒度局部动态；2) 长期记忆专家通过轻量测试时微调将情节历史存储在外部扩散权重中；3) 空间长期记忆专家强制几何和空间一致性。这种组合设计避免模式坍缩且无需二次成本即可扩展到长上下文。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准上，该方法在时间一致性、过去观察回忆和导航性能方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了构建和运行记忆增强扩散世界模型的新范式，有效解决了长上下文场景下的记忆-保真度权衡问题。

Abstract: World models aim to predict plausible futures consistent with past observations, a capability central to planning and decision-making in reinforcement learning. Yet, existing architectures face a fundamental memory trade-off: transformers preserve local detail but are bottlenecked by quadratic attention, while recurrent and state-space models scale more efficiently but compress history at the cost of fidelity. To overcome this trade-off, we suggest decoupling future-past consistency from any single architecture and instead leveraging a set of specialized experts. We introduce a diffusion-based framework that integrates heterogeneous memory models through a contrastive product-of-experts formulation. Our approach instantiates three complementary roles: a short-term memory expert that captures fine local dynamics, a long-term memory expert that stores episodic history in external diffusion weights via lightweight test-time finetuning, and a spatial long-term memory expert that enforces geometric and spatial coherence. This compositional design avoids mode collapse and scales to long contexts without incurring a quadratic cost. Across simulated and real-world benchmarks, our method improves temporal consistency, recall of past observations, and navigation performance, establishing a novel paradigm for building and operating memory-augmented diffusion world models.

</details>


### [121] [Set-Valued Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19830)
*Laura Fuentes-Vicente,Mathieu Even,Gaëlle Dormion,Antoine Chambaz,Uri Shalit,Julie Josse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多治疗场景的集值策略学习范式，输出一组可行治疗而非单一推荐，通过内在不确定性量化（预测集大小反映决策模糊度）和保形策略学习（结合噪声标签的随机注入方法保证边际覆盖），实现了性能与可靠性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点值治疗策略对估计不确定性、模型设定和有限样本变异性高度敏感，且无法提供推荐动作的置信度指导，亟需一种能内在地量化决策不确定性的方法。

Method: 1) 将学习-延迟框架扩展到多治疗场景，提出greatest Lower Bound方法；2) 提出保形策略学习，弥合未观测真实最优治疗与估计策略间的差距；3) 借鉴噪声标签文献，开发随机注入方法，在不假设黑箱最优治疗规则的情况下保证边际覆盖。

Result: 在合成数据和体外受精(IVF)真实应用中，该方法在保持性能的同时自然融入临床考量，有效平衡了表现与可靠性。

Conclusion: 集值策略学习通过输出治疗集合并量化决策模糊度，为多治疗场景下的临床决策提供了更稳健且可操作的方案，尤其适用于对不确定性敏感的关键医疗应用。

Abstract: Conventional treatment policies map patient covariates to a single recommended intervention in order to maximize expected clinical outcomes. Although a rich body of causal inference methods has been developed to estimate such policies, point-valued recommendations can be highly sensitive to estimation uncertainty, model specification, and finite-sample variability, while typically providing little guidance about how confident one should be in the recommended action. In this work, we propose a set-valued policy learning paradigm for the multiple-treatment setting, in which policies output a set of plausible treatments rather than a single recommendation. This formulation enables intrinsic uncertainty quantification, with the size of the predicted set reflecting the degree of decision ambiguity. We extend the learning-to-defer framework to multiple treatments via a novel \textit{greatest Lower Bound} method, and introduce \textit{conformal policy learning}, which bridges the gap between unobserved ground-truth optimal treatments and estimated optimal treatment rules. Drawing on insights from the noisy-label literature, we develop a randomness-injection approach that guarantees marginal coverage without requiring assumptions on underlying black-box optimal treatment rules. Through experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application to In-Vitro Fertilization (IVF), we demonstrate that our methods produce robust and actionable policies that naturally incorporate clinical considerations while effectively balancing performance and reliability.

</details>


### [122] [When Does Model Collapse Occur in Structured Interactive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20151)
*Yuchen Wu,Kangjie Zhou,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式AI在交互式学习环境（模型间通过合成数据相互影响）中的性能退化问题，发现模型崩溃的发生与否关键取决于模型交互图的拓扑结构，并给出了充要条件及理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注单模型在自身生成数据上训练导致的模型崩溃问题，未能捕捉多模型交互环境下的模型性能表现。然而现实中的生成式AI系统往往存在多个模型相互交互、共享合成数据的复杂情况，建立可靠的统计推断方法成为重要开放问题。

Method: 利用有向图对模型交互模式进行形式化建模；推导了模型崩溃发生的显式充要条件；针对线性回归建立了有限样本结果，对一般M-估计量建立了渐近保证；通过大量数值实验验证理论发现。

Result: 模型崩溃的发生关键取决于交互图的拓扑结构；给出了模型崩溃是否发生的明确充要条件；在线性回归和一般M-估计量下分别提供了有限样本和渐近的理论保证。

Conclusion: 多模型交互环境中的模型崩溃与否由交互图的拓扑结构决定，该发现填补了先前单模型研究的空白，为理解和缓解生成式AI系统在交互式学习环境中的退化问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has given rise to an interactive learning environment, where model parameters are continuously updated using not only data generated by natural processes, but also synthetic outputs produced by other models. This paradigm introduces two major challenges: (1) training data are no longer drawn exclusively from the target population, undermining a core assumption of classical statistical learning, and (2) model training processes become inherently correlated, as models interact with one another through repeated exposure to each other's synthetic outputs in a potentially complex manner. Establishing reliable statistical inference in such structured interactive learning environments therefore remains an important open problem. In particular, there is growing concern about model collapse, a phenomenon in which the performance of generative models progressively degrades as they are trained on synthetic data produced by earlier model generations.
  Prior work on model collapse primarily focuses on a single model trained on its own output, failing to capture model performance in multi-model interactive settings. In this work, we fill this gap by investigating the performance of generative models in an interactive learning environment with general interaction patterns. In particular, we formalize model interactions using directed graphs and show that the occurrence of model collapse depends critically on the topology of the interaction graph. We further derive an explicit necessary and sufficient condition characterizing when model collapse occurs, and establish finite-sample results for linear regression and asymptotic guarantees for general M-estimators. We support our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [123] [DynaTrain: Fast Online Parallelism Switching for Elastic LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18815)
*Yuanqing Wang,Yuchen Zhang,Hao Lin,Junhao Hu,Chunyang Zhu,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Zhi Yang,Daning Cheng,Yunquan Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTrain是一个分布式训练系统，通过虚拟参数空间(VPS)抽象实现亚秒级在线重配置，支持任意多维并行性动态调整，在70B密集模型和235B MoE模型上重配置时间分别低于2秒和4.36秒，性能优于现有方案三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练本质上是动态的（资源波动、RLHF阶段变化、集群弹性），但现有训练框架基于静态执行模型构建，难以应对最优并行布局的持续变化。

Method: 提出虚拟参数空间(VPS)抽象统一分布式训练状态为逻辑坐标空间，将并行配置映射为确定性映射，复杂转换简化为几何交集；基于VPS构建状态路由与转换层实现rank本地传输（内存感知、无死锁调度），弹性设备管理器将新世界构建与训练重叠以掩盖拓扑变化开销。

Result: 在高达235B参数的密集和MoE模型上，DynaTrain重配置70B密集模型不到2秒，235B MoE模型4.36秒，比现有基于检查点和弹性系统快三个数量级，同时保持正确性。

Conclusion: DynaTrain通过VPS抽象和在线重配置机制，有效解决了动态训练环境下的并行布局优化难题，实现了亚秒级重配置和数量级性能提升。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) training is inherently dynamic: resource fluctuations, RLHF phase shifts, and cluster elasticity continually reshape the optimal parallelism layout, posing a significant challenge to existing training frameworks built around a static execution model. We present DynaTrain, a distributed training system for sub-second, online reconfiguration across arbitrary multi-dimensional parallelism. At its core, we propose a Virtual Parameter Space (VPS) abstraction that unifies all distributed training states under one logical coordinate space, turning any parallelism configuration into a deterministic mapping and collapsing complex transition into manageable geometric intersections. On top of VPS, a state routing-and-transition layer executes rank-local transfers under a memory-aware, deadlock-free schedule, and an Elastic Device Manager overlaps new-world construction with ongoing training to mask topology-change cost. On dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, DynaTrain reconfigures a 70B dense model in under 2s and a 235B MoE model in 4.36s, outperforming state-of-the-art checkpoint-based and elastic systems by up to three orders of magnitude while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [124] [Emergence of Frontier Superposition: Möbius attractor and Cascade Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18820)
*Hongyu Gu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在Erdős-Rényi图的广度优先可达性任务中，通过Möbius吸引子（解决置换对称鞍点问题）与级联监督（解决梯度衰减问题）的组合，梯度下降能够发现叠加推理状态，使Transformer在定深度前向传播中并行执行推理前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu等人(2025)手工构造了单残差流中的等权重广度优先叠加状态用于图可达性推理，但梯度下降能否在置换对称鞍点环境下找到该目标状态尚不清楚。本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 架构上，在树状机制下利用S_n-对称性将层间动力学约简为1D Möbius映射，其零点集为包含等权重叠加态的余维一全局最优流形。监督上，提出级联监督损失类，其反向传播同时提供选择性自举、跨深度梯度持久性和逐步骤判别性。相比之下，端到端监督在深度D中内部梯度以(np)^{-(D-c-2)/2}衰减，无法到达最优流形。

Result: 理论预测最终步余弦值端到端为0.35、级联为0.71（深度D=3）；实验验证分别为0.37和0.69，每步误差在0.02以内。

Conclusion: Möbius吸引子与级联监督的组合是叠加推理涌现的充分条件，解决了置换对称鞍点和梯度衰减两个关键障碍。

Abstract: Superposition allows Transformers to reason in depth, carrying an entire reasoning frontier in parallel through a bounded-depth forward pass instead of unrolling serial chain-of-thought tokens. While Zhu et al. (2025) hand-crafted an equal-weight breadth-first frontier in a single residual stream for graph reachability, it remained open whether gradient descent could ever find this target amidst permutation-symmetric saddles.
  We close this gap on Reachability-by-Superposition over Erdős-Rényi graphs by isolating architectural and supervisional contributions. Architecturally, we identify a Möbius attractor: under $S_n$-symmetry in the tree regime, layerwise dynamics reduce to a 1D Möbius map whose zero set is a codimension-one manifold of global optima containing the equal-weight superposition state.
  On the supervision side, we identify Cascade Supervision: a loss class whose backward pass simultaneously delivers (A) selectivity bootstrap, (B) gradient persistence across depth, and (C) per-step discrimination (e.g., \mathcal{L}_{sup} and \mathcal{L}_{node}). End-to-end supervision fails condition (B) and is provably insufficient: internal gradients at layer c decay as (np)^{-(D-c-2)/2} in the graph fan-out and stall before the manifold is reached.
  Our thesis: Möbius attractor + Cascade Supervision = emergence of superposition reasoning. The parameter-free decay law predicts a final-step cosine of 0.35 vs. 0.71 (end-to-end vs. cascade) at depth D=3; experiments confirm 0.37 vs. 0.69, matching within 0.02 at every step.

</details>


### [125] [Hybrid-LoRA: Bridging Full Fine-Tuning and Low-Rank Adaptation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18822)
*Chengqian Zhang,Wei Zhu,Kyumin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid-LoRA框架，通过选择性全微调对低秩适配敏感的小部分模块，其余模块使用LoRA，在10%全微调模块预算下接近全微调性能，平均比最优基线提升4.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR后训练中使用全微调需要大量GPU内存和高训练成本，而PEFT方法（如LoRA）虽能降低计算成本，但在复杂推理任务的后训练中与全微调存在明显性能差距。

Method: 提出Hybrid-LoRA框架，选择性对少量对低秩适配敏感的模块进行全微调，其余组件使用LoRA适配；引入Hybrid-LoRA Score对候选模块进行排序，在固定参数预算下选择最优的模块进行全微调。

Result: 在10%全微调模块预算下接近全微调性能，持续优于四种最先进PEFT后训练基线，最高提升5.65%，平均提升4.36%。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LoRA通过混合全微调与LoRA的策略，在显著降低训练成本的同时，有效缩小了PEFT与全微调在复杂推理任务后训练中的性能差距。

Abstract: Post-training has become essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to complex downstream behaviors, including instruction following, preference alignment, and multi-step reasoning. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently emerged as a particularly effective post-training paradigm for improving reasoning capabilities, with critic-free algorithms such as GRPO and GSPO enabling scalable optimization. However, RLVR post-training with full fine-tuning (FFT) requires substantial GPU memory and incurs high training costs. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), effectively reduce computational costs, they often suffer from a noticeable performance gap compared to full fine-tuning in post-training for complex reasoning tasks. In this paper, we propose Hybrid-LoRA, an efficient hybrid post-training framework that selectively applies full fine-tuning to a small subset of modules less suited to low-rank adaptation, while adapting the remaining components with LoRA. We introduce a novel Hybrid-LoRA Score to rank candidate modules according to their sensitivity to low-rank adaptation under a fixed parameter budget. Experiments show that Hybrid-LoRA closely matches full fine-tuning performance under a 10% full fine-tuning module budget, with the remaining candidate modules adapted by LoRA, consistently outperforming four state-of-the-art PEFT post-training baselines, achieving improvements of up to 5.65% and on average 4.36% over the best baseline.

</details>
